import { defineConfig, loadEnv } from 'vite'
import vue from '@vitejs/plugin-vue'

// https://vitejs.dev/config/
export default ({ mode }) => {
  return defineConfig({
    base: loadEnv(mode, process.cwd()).VITE_BASE_URL,
    plugins: [vue()],
    server: {
      https: true,
      host: '0.0.0.0',
    },
    resolve: {
      extensions: ['.js', '.jsx', '.ts', '.tsx', '.json', '.vue'],
    },
  })
}

<body>
    <canvas id="mainCanvas"></canvas>

    <div id="startOverlay">
        <div class="title">✦ 星河入梦 ✦</div>
        <div class="sub">花火 · 许愿 · 祝福</div>
        <button id="startBtn">开 始</button>
        <div class="hint">点击按钮，开启听觉之旅</div>
    </div>

    <div id="info">✦ 华灯 · 陨星 · 星河 ✦</div>
    <div class="status-tip" id="statusTip"></div>
    <button id="sound-toggle">🔇 音效已静音</button>

    <script>
        // ================================================================
        // sound-configurator.js (完整内联 v1.2.0 — 支持 ignoreEndTime)
        // ================================================================
        (function (global) {
            'use strict';

            const DEFAULT_CONFIG = {
                sounds: {
                    heartbeat: {
                        url: "https://cdn.jsdmirror.com/gh/DyWriteCode/DyWriteCode.github.io/TEST_FUNCION/AUDIO/heart.mp3",
                        start: 0.0,
                        end: 2.0,
                        volume: 1
                    },
                    city: {
                        url: 'https://cdn.jsdmirror.com/gh/DyWriteCode/DyWriteCode.github.io/TEST_FUNCION/AUDIO/city.mp3',
                        start: 0.0,
                        end: 6.0,
                        volume: 1
                    },
                    space: {
                        url: 'https://cdn.jsdmirror.com/gh/DyWriteCode/DyWriteCode.github.io/TEST_FUNCION/AUDIO/space.mp3',
                        start: 0.0,
                        end: 4.0,
                        volume: 1
                    },
                    meteor: {
                        url: 'https://cdn.jsdmirror.com/gh/DyWriteCode/DyWriteCode.github.io/TEST_FUNCION/AUDIO/meteor.mp3',
                        start: 0.0,
                        end: 3.5,
                    },
                    firework_launch: {
                        url: 'https://cdn.jsdmirror.com/gh/DyWriteCode/DyWriteCode.github.io/TEST_FUNCION/AUDIO/firework_go.mp3',
                        start: 0.0,
                        end: 1.8,
                    },
                    firework_explode: {
                        url: 'https://cdn.jsdmirror.com/gh/DyWriteCode/DyWriteCode.github.io/TEST_FUNCION/AUDIO/firework_boom.mp3',
                        start: 0.0,
                        end: 2.5,
                    },
                    birthday: {
                        url: 'https://cdn.jsdmirror.com/gh/DyWriteCode/DyWriteCode.github.io/TEST_FUNCION/AUDIO/birthday.mp3',
                        start: 0.0,
                        end: 12.0,
                    },
                },
                meteor: {
                    panStart: -0.8,
                    panEnd: 0.8,
                    volumeStart: 0.5,
                    volumeEnd: 0.2,
                    duration: 3.5,
                },
                firework: {
                    launchDuration: 1.5,
                    explodeDelay: 0.6,
                    panSpread: 0.6,
                },
            };

            // ============================================================
            // AudioEngine — 增加 ignoreEndTime 参数
            // ============================================================
            class AudioEngine {
                constructor() {
                    this.ctx = null;
                    this.buffers = {};
                    this.activeSources = new Map();
                    this.sourceIdCounter = 0;
                    this.isPreloading = false;
                    this._muted = false;
                }

                getContext() {
                    if (!this.ctx) {
                        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
                    }
                    return this.ctx;
                }

                async preloadAll(soundConfigs) {
                    this.isPreloading = true;
                    const entries = Object.entries(soundConfigs);
                    await Promise.all(entries.map(([key, cfg]) => {
                        if (cfg.url) {
                            return this.loadSound(key, cfg.url).catch(err => {
                                console.warn(`预加载 ${key} 失败，使用合成后备音`, err);
                                return null;
                            });
                        }
                        return Promise.resolve();
                    }));
                    this.isPreloading = false;
                    console.log('✅ 所有音频预加载完成');
                }

                async loadSound(key, url) {
                    if (this.buffers[key]) return this.buffers[key];
                    try {
                        const resp = await fetch(url);
                        if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${resp.status}`);
                        const arrayBuffer = await resp.arrayBuffer();
                        const ctx = this.getContext();
                        const audioBuffer = await ctx.decodeAudioData(arrayBuffer);
                        this.buffers[key] = audioBuffer;
                        return audioBuffer;
                    } catch (err) {
                        console.warn(`⚠️ 加载音频 "${key}" 失败 (${url})，使用合成音代替`, err);
                        const synth = this._synthesizeFallback(key);
                        this.buffers[key] = synth;
                        return synth;
                    }
                }

                // ----- 合成后备音 (包含生日快乐) -----
                _synthesizeFallback(key) {
                    const ctx = this.getContext();
                    const sr = ctx.sampleRate;
                    const synths = {
                        birthday: () => {
                            const dur = 12.0;
                            const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                            const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                            const freq = {
                                C4: 261.63,
                                D4: 293.66,
                                E4: 329.63,
                                F4: 349.23,
                                G4: 392.00,
                                A4: 440.00,
                                B4: 493.88,
                                C5: 523.25,
                                D5: 587.33,
                                E5: 659.25,
                                F5: 698.46,
                                G5: 783.99,
                                A5: 880.00,
                                B5: 987.77
                            };
                            const melody = [
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['A4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['C5', 0.40, 0.75],
                                ['B4', 0.40, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['A4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['D5', 0.40, 0.75],
                                ['C5', 0.40, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['G4', 0.30, 0.7],
                                ['E5', 0.30, 0.75],
                                ['C5', 0.40, 0.75],
                                ['B4', 0.40, 0.7],
                                ['A4', 0.40, 0.7],
                                ['F5', 0.30, 0.75],
                                ['F5', 0.30, 0.75],
                                ['E5', 0.30, 0.75],
                                ['C5', 0.40, 0.75],
                                ['D5', 0.40, 0.75],
                                ['C5', 0.50, 0.7]
                            ];
                            let currentTime = 0;
                            const gap = 0.015;
                            for (const [note, duration, volume] of melody) {
                                const f = freq[note] || 440;
                                const start = currentTime;
                                const end = start + duration;
                                const samplesStart = Math.floor(start * sr);
                                const samplesEnd = Math.floor(end * sr);
                                for (let i = samplesStart; i < Math.min(samplesEnd, d.length); i++) {
                                    const t = (i / sr) - start;
                                    let env = 1;
                                    if (t < 0.02) env = t / 0.02;
                                    else if (t > duration - 0.02) env = (duration - t) / 0.02;
                                    const val = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f * t) * 0.6 +
                                        Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f * 2 * t) * 0.1 +
                                        Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f * 3 * t) * 0.05;
                                    d[i] += val * env * volume * 0.7;
                                }
                                currentTime = end + gap;
                            }
                            let max = 0;
                            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++)
                                if (Math.abs(d[i]) > max) max = Math.abs(d[i]);
                            if (max > 0.01)
                                for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] /= max * 1.05;
                            return buf;
                        },
                        heartbeat: () => {
                            const dur = 2.0;
                            const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                            const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) {
                                const t = i / sr;
                                let env = 0;
                                if (t < 0.15) env = Math.sin(t / 0.15 * Math.PI) * 0.8;
                                else if (t < 0.3) env = Math.sin((t - 0.15) / 0.15 * Math.PI) * 0.4;
                                else if (t > 1.0 && t < 1.15) env = Math.sin((t - 1.0) / 0.15 * Math.PI) * 0.7;
                                else if (t > 1.15 && t < 1.3) env = Math.sin((t - 1.15) / 0.15 * Math.PI) * 0.3;
                                const freq = 60 + 30 * Math.sin(t * 8);
                                d[i] = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * freq * t) * env * 0.5;
                            }
                            return buf;
                        },
                        city: () => {
                            const dur = 6.0;
                            const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                            const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) {
                                const t = i / sr;
                                const noise = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.3;
                                const hum = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 120 * t) * 0.1;
                                const ring = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 800 * t) * Math.exp(-t * 0.5) * 0.05;
                                const spike = (Math.random() > 0.995) ? (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.2 : 0;
                                const env = Math.exp(-t * 0.08) * 0.8 + 0.2;
                                d[i] = (noise + hum + ring + spike) * env * 0.6;
                            }
                            return buf;
                        },
                        space: () => {
                            const dur = 4.0;
                            const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                            const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) {
                                const t = i / sr;
                                const freq = 80 + 40 * Math.sin(t * 0.5);
                                const v = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * freq * t) * 0.3 +
                                    Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * (freq * 1.5) * t) * 0.1;
                                d[i] = v * Math.exp(-t * 0.05) * 0.6;
                            }
                            return buf;
                        },
                        meteor: () => {
                            const dur = 3.5;
                            const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                            const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) {
                                const t = i / sr;
                                const freq = 800 - t * 200 + Math.sin(t * 30) * 50;
                                const env = Math.exp(-t * 1.2) * 0.7 + 0.1;
                                const noise = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.15 * env;
                                const tone = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * freq * t) * env * 0.5;
                                d[i] = (tone + noise) * 0.5;
                            }
                            return buf;
                        },
                        firework_launch: () => {
                            const dur = 1.8;
                            const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                            const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) {
                                const t = i / sr;
                                const freq = 150 + t * 600;
                                const env = Math.sin(t / dur * Math.PI) * 0.8;
                                const noise = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.2 * env;
                                const tone = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * freq * t) * env * 0.5;
                                d[i] = (tone + noise) * 0.5;
                            }
                            return buf;
                        },
                        firework_explode: () => {
                            const dur = 2.5;
                            const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                            const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                            for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) {
                                const t = i / sr;
                                const env = Math.exp(-t * 1.8) * 0.9;
                                const noise = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.6 * env;
                                const low = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 80 * t) * env * 0.3;
                                const mid = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 300 * t) * Math.exp(-t * 2.5) * 0.2;
                                d[i] = (noise + low + mid) * 0.6;
                            }
                            return buf;
                        },
                    };
                    const gen = synths[key] || (() => {
                        const dur = 2.0;
                        const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, sr * dur, sr);
                        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                        for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) {
                            const t = i / sr;
                            d[i] = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 440 * t) * Math.exp(-t * 0.5) * 0.3;
                        }
                        return buf;
                    });
                    return gen();
                }

                // ----- playSound 增加 ignoreEndTime 参数 -----
                async playSound(key, startTime, endTime, volume = 0.8, pan = 0, loop = false, ignoreEndTime = false) {
                    if (this._muted) return null;
                    const buffer = await this.loadSound(key, key);
                    if (!buffer) return null;
                    const ctx = this.getContext();
                    const source = ctx.createBufferSource();
                    source.buffer = buffer;
                    const gainNode = ctx.createGain();
                    gainNode.gain.value = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, volume));
                    const panner = ctx.createStereoPanner();
                    panner.pan.value = Math.min(1, Math.max(-1, pan));
                    source.connect(gainNode);
                    gainNode.connect(panner);
                    panner.connect(ctx.destination);

                    const actualStart = Math.min(Math.max(0, startTime || 0), buffer.duration - 0.05);
                    const actualEnd = ignoreEndTime ?
                        buffer.duration :
                        Math.min(Math.max(actualStart + 0.05, endTime || buffer.duration), buffer.duration);
                    const duration = actualEnd - actualStart;

                    if (loop) {
                        source.loop = true;
                        source.loopStart = actualStart;
                        source.loopEnd = actualEnd;
                    }
                    source.start(0, actualStart, loop ? undefined : duration);

                    const id = ++this.sourceIdCounter;
                    const entry = {
                        id,
                        key,
                        source,
                        gainNode,
                        panner,
                        isPlaying: true,
                        loop: loop,
                        stop: () => {
                            try { source.stop(); } catch (_) { }
                            entry.isPlaying = false;
                            this.activeSources.delete(id);
                        },
                    };
                    this.activeSources.set(id, entry);
                    source.onended = () => {
                        if (!loop) {
                            entry.isPlaying = false;
                            this.activeSources.delete(id);
                        }
                    };
                    return entry;
                }

                stopSound(id) {
                    const entry = this.activeSources.get(id);
                    if (entry) {
                        entry.stop();
                        this.activeSources.delete(id);
                        return true;
                    }
                    return false;
                }

                stopAll() {
                    for (const [id, entry] of this.activeSources) {
                        entry.stop();
                    }
                    this.activeSources.clear();
                }

                setVolume(id, vol) {
                    const entry = this.activeSources.get(id);
                    if (entry) {
                        entry.gainNode.gain.value = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, vol));
                    }
                }

                setPan(id, pan) {
                    const entry = this.activeSources.get(id);
                    if (entry) {
                        entry.panner.pan.value = Math.min(1, Math.max(-1, pan));
                    }
                }

                isPlaying(id) {
                    const entry = this.activeSources.get(id);
                    return entry ? entry.isPlaying : false;
                }

                setMuted(muted) {
                    this._muted = muted;
                    if (muted) this.stopAll();
                }

                isMuted() { return this._muted; }
            }

            // ============================================================
            // MeteorManager / FireworkManager (保持不变)
            // ============================================================
            class MeteorManager {
                constructor(engine, config) {
                    this.engine = engine;
                    this.config = config;
                    this.meteors = [];
                    this.idCounter = 0;
                    this.onUpdate = null;
                    this.animFrame = null;
                    this.isRunning = false;
                }

                async addMeteor(panStart, panEnd, volStart, volEnd, duration) {
                    const cfg = this.config.meteor;
                    const startPan = panStart ?? cfg.panStart;
                    const endPan = panEnd ?? cfg.panEnd;
                    const startVol = volStart ?? cfg.volumeStart;
                    const endVol = volEnd ?? cfg.volumeEnd;
                    const dur = Math.min(duration ?? cfg.duration, this.config.sounds.meteor.end - this.config.sounds
                        .meteor.start || 3.5);
                    const id = ++this.idCounter;
                    const meteor = {
                        id,
                        panStart: startPan,
                        panEnd: endPan,
                        volStart: startVol,
                        volEnd: endVol,
                        duration: dur,
                        elapsed: 0,
                        isPlaying: false,
                        sourceId: null,
                        progress: 0,
                        active: true,
                    };
                    this.meteors.push(meteor);
                    this._startAnimation();
                    this._updateUI();
                    return meteor;
                }

                async playMeteor(meteor) {
                    if (meteor.isPlaying || this.engine.isMuted()) return;
                    const cfg = this.config.sounds.meteor;
                    const entry = await this.engine.playSound(
                        'meteor',
                        cfg.start,
                        cfg.end,
                        meteor.volStart,
                        meteor.panStart,
                        false, // 不循环
                        true // ★ ignoreEndTime = true，播放完整音频
                    );
                    if (entry) {
                        meteor.sourceId = entry.id;
                        meteor.isPlaying = true;
                        meteor.elapsed = 0;
                        meteor.progress = 0;
                        this._updateUI();
                    }
                }

                stopMeteor(meteor) {
                    if (meteor.sourceId) {
                        this.engine.stopSound(meteor.sourceId);
                        meteor.sourceId = null;
                    }
                    meteor.isPlaying = false;
                    this._updateUI();
                }

                removeMeteor(meteor) {
                    this.stopMeteor(meteor);
                    meteor.active = false;
                    this.meteors = this.meteors.filter(m => m !== meteor);
                    if (this.meteors.length === 0) this._stopAnimation();
                    this._updateUI();
                }

                clearAll() {
                    for (const m of this.meteors) this.stopMeteor(m);
                    this.meteors = [];
                    this._stopAnimation();
                    this._updateUI();
                }

                _startAnimation() {
                    if (this.isRunning) return;
                    this.isRunning = true;
                    const step = () => {
                        if (!this.isRunning) return;
                        let hasActive = false;
                        for (const m of this.meteors) {
                            if (!m.isPlaying) continue;
                            hasActive = true;
                            m.elapsed += 0.04;
                            m.progress = Math.min(1, m.elapsed / m.duration);
                            const pan = m.panStart + (m.panEnd - m.panStart) * m.progress;
                            const vol = m.volStart + (m.volEnd - m.volStart) * m.progress;
                            this.engine.setPan(m.sourceId, pan);
                            this.engine.setVolume(m.sourceId, vol);
                            if (m.progress >= 1) this.stopMeteor(m);
                        }
                        this._updateUI();
                        if (hasActive || this.meteors.some(m => m.isPlaying)) {
                            this.animFrame = requestAnimationFrame(step);
                        } else {
                            this.isRunning = false;
                            this.animFrame = null;
                        }
                    };
                    this.animFrame = requestAnimationFrame(step);
                }

                _stopAnimation() {
                    this.isRunning = false;
                    if (this.animFrame) {
                        cancelAnimationFrame(this.animFrame);
                        this.animFrame = null;
                    }
                }

                _updateUI() {
                    if (this.onUpdate) this.onUpdate(this.meteors);
                }

                get count() { return this.meteors.length; }
            }

            class FireworkManager {
                constructor(engine, config) {
                    this.engine = engine;
                    this.config = config;
                    this.fireworks = [];
                    this.idCounter = 0;
                    this.onUpdate = null;
                    this.timeouts = [];
                }

                async addFirework() {
                    if (this.engine.isMuted()) return null;
                    const id = ++this.idCounter;
                    const fw = {
                        id,
                        isPlaying: false,
                        launchId: null,
                        explodeId: null,
                        active: true,
                        phase: 'idle',
                    };
                    this.fireworks.push(fw);
                    this._updateUI();
                    await this._playFirework(fw);
                    return fw;
                }

                async _playFirework(fw) {
                    if (fw.isPlaying || this.engine.isMuted()) return;
                    fw.isPlaying = true;
                    fw.phase = 'launching';
                    this._updateUI();

                    const launchCfg = this.config.sounds.firework_launch;
                    const launchEntry = await this.engine.playSound(
                        'firework_launch',
                        launchCfg.start,
                        launchCfg.end,
                        0.7,
                        0,
                        false,
                        false
                    );
                    fw.launchId = launchEntry?.id || null;

                    let launchProgress = 0;
                    const launchInterval = setInterval(() => {
                        if (!fw.active || !fw.isPlaying || this.engine.isMuted()) {
                            clearInterval(launchInterval);
                            return;
                        }
                        launchProgress += 0.05;
                        const vol = 0.4 + launchProgress * 0.5;
                        const pan = Math.sin(launchProgress * 3) * 0.2;
                        if (fw.launchId) {
                            this.engine.setVolume(fw.launchId, Math.min(1, vol));
                            this.engine.setPan(fw.launchId, pan);
                        }
                        if (launchProgress >= 1) clearInterval(launchInterval);
                    }, 50);

                    const delay = this.config.firework.explodeDelay || 0.6;
                    const timeoutId = setTimeout(async () => {
                        if (!fw.active || !fw.isPlaying || this.engine.isMuted()) return;
                        if (fw.launchId) {
                            this.engine.stopSound(fw.launchId);
                            fw.launchId = null;
                        }
                        fw.phase = 'exploding';
                        this._updateUI();
                        const explodeCfg = this.config.sounds.firework_explode;
                        const panSpread = this.config.firework.panSpread || 0.6;
                        const pan = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * panSpread;
                        const explodeEntry = await this.engine.playSound(
                            'firework_explode',
                            explodeCfg.start,
                            explodeCfg.end,
                            0.9,
                            pan,
                            false,
                            false
                        );
                        fw.explodeId = explodeEntry?.id || null;
                        if (explodeEntry) {
                            const checkDone = () => {
                                if (!fw.active || this.engine.isMuted()) return;
                                if (!this.engine.isPlaying(explodeEntry.id)) {
                                    fw.isPlaying = false;
                                    fw.phase = 'done';
                                    this._updateUI();
                                } else {
                                    setTimeout(checkDone, 200);
                                }
                            };
                            setTimeout(checkDone, 100);
                        } else {
                            fw.isPlaying = false;
                            fw.phase = 'done';
                            this._updateUI();
                        }
                    }, delay * 1000);

                    this.timeouts.push(timeoutId);
                    fw._cleanup = () => {
                        clearInterval(launchInterval);
                        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                        if (fw.launchId) this.engine.stopSound(fw.launchId);
                        if (fw.explodeId) this.engine.stopSound(fw.explodeId);
                        fw.launchId = null;
                        fw.explodeId = null;
                        fw.isPlaying = false;
                    };
                }

                stopFirework(fw) {
                    if (fw._cleanup) fw._cleanup();
                    fw.isPlaying = false;
                    fw.phase = 'idle';
                    this._updateUI();
                }

                removeFirework(fw) {
                    this.stopFirework(fw);
                    fw.active = false;
                    this.fireworks = this.fireworks.filter(f => f !== fw);
                    this._updateUI();
                }

                clearAll() {
                    for (const fw of this.fireworks) this.stopFirework(fw);
                    this.fireworks = [];
                    this.timeouts.forEach(t => clearTimeout(t));
                    this.timeouts = [];
                    this._updateUI();
                }

                get count() { return this.fireworks.length; }

                _updateUI() {
                    if (this.onUpdate) this.onUpdate(this.fireworks);
                }
            }

            async function createSoundSystem(customConfig = {}) {
                const config = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_CONFIG));
                if (customConfig.sounds) {
                    for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(customConfig.sounds)) {
                        if (config.sounds[key]) {
                            Object.assign(config.sounds[key], val);
                        } else {
                            config.sounds[key] = val;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (customConfig.meteor) {
                    Object.assign(config.meteor, customConfig.meteor);
                }
                if (customConfig.firework) {
                    Object.assign(config.firework, customConfig.firework);
                }

                const engine = new AudioEngine();
                await engine.preloadAll(config.sounds);

                const meteorManager = new MeteorManager(engine, config);
                const fireworkManager = new FireworkManager(engine, config);

                return {
                    engine,
                    meteorManager,
                    fireworkManager,
                    config,
                };
            }

            const SoundSystem = {
                version: '1.2.0',
                DEFAULT_CONFIG,
                AudioEngine,
                MeteorManager,
                FireworkManager,
                create: createSoundSystem,
            };

            if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
                window.SoundSystem = SoundSystem;
            }
            if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
                module.exports = SoundSystem;
                module.exports.default = SoundSystem;
            }
            if (typeof define === 'function' && define.amd) {
                define([], function () { return SoundSystem; });
            }
            return SoundSystem;
        })(this);
        console.log('🔊 声音系统已加载 (sound-configurator.js v1.2.0 — 支持 ignoreEndTime)');
    </script>

    <script>
        (function () {
            'use strict';

            // ================================================================
            // 全局配置
            // ================================================================
            const CONFIG_MASTER = {
                showerTriggerRatio: 0.5,
                firstCloseDuration: 3500,
                firstHoldDuration: 800,
                firstOpenDuration: 1500,
                fireworksDuration: 28000,
                smokeWaitDuration: 2800,
                cakeTransitionDuration: 1200,
                cakeCloseDuration: 3500,
                cakeHoldDuration: 2000,
                cakeOpenDuration: 1500,
            };

            // ================================================================
            // 工具函数 (略)
            // ================================================================
            function clamp(v, a, b) { return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v)); }

            function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }

            function rand(a, b) { return Math.random() * (b - a) + a; }

            function randInt(a, b) { return Math.floor(rand(a, b + 1)); }

            function easeOutCubic(t) { return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3); }

            function easeInOutQuad(t) { return t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 2) / 2; }

            function easeInOutSine(t) { return -(Math.cos(Math.PI * t) - 1) / 2; }

            function easeOutQuad(t) { return 1 - (1 - t) * (1 - t); }

            function easeInOutCubic(t) { return t < 0.5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 3) / 2; }

            function smoothstep(e0, e1, x) { const t = clamp((x - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0, 1); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); }

            function hash(x, y) {
                let h = x * 374761393 + y * 668265263;
                h = (h ^ (h >> 13)) * 1274126177;
                return ((h ^ (h >> 16)) & 0x7fffffff) / 0x7fffffff;
            }

            function lighten(hex, a) {
                let r = parseInt(hex.slice(1, 3), 16),
                    g = parseInt(hex.slice(3, 5), 16),
                    b = parseInt(hex.slice(5, 7), 16);
                r = Math.min(255, r + a);
                g = Math.min(255, g + a);
                b = Math.min(255, b + a);
                return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
            }

            function darken(hex, a) {
                let r = parseInt(hex.slice(1, 3), 16),
                    g = parseInt(hex.slice(3, 5), 16),
                    b = parseInt(hex.slice(5, 7), 16);
                r = Math.max(0, r - a);
                g = Math.max(0, g - a);
                b = Math.max(0, b - a);
                return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
            }

            function hexToRgb(hex) {
                const result = /^#?([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})$/i.exec(hex);
                return result ? { r: parseInt(result[1], 16), g: parseInt(result[2], 16), b: parseInt(result[3], 16) } : {
                    r: 0,
                    g: 0,
                    b: 0
                };
            }

            function rgbToHex(r, g, b) {
                return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => clamp(Math.round(c), 0, 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
            }

            function lightenColor(hex, amount) {
                const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(hex);
                return rgbToHex(Math.min(255, r + amount), Math.min(255, g + amount), Math.min(255, b + amount));
            }

            function darkenColor(hex, amount) {
                const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(hex);
                return rgbToHex(Math.max(0, r - amount), Math.max(0, g - amount), Math.max(0, b - amount));
            }

            function roundRect(ctx, x, y, w, h, r) {
                r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
                ctx.lineTo(x + w - r, y);
                ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);
                ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r);
                ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);
                ctx.lineTo(x + r, y + h);
                ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);
                ctx.lineTo(x, y + r);
                ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y);
                ctx.closePath();
            }

            // ================================================================
            // Canvas 初始化
            // ================================================================
            const canvas = document.getElementById('mainCanvas');
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: true });
            const infoEl = document.getElementById('info');
            const statusTip = document.getElementById('statusTip');
            const soundToggle = document.getElementById('sound-toggle');
            const startOverlay = document.getElementById('startOverlay');
            const startBtn = document.getElementById('startBtn');
            let W, H;

            // ================================================================
            // ★ 修复：将 PARTICLE_CFG 定义提前，避免在 resize 中访问时未初始化
            // ================================================================
            const PARTICLE_CFG = {
                flyDuration: 2000,
                fontSizeMin: 24,
                fontSizeMax: 30,
                textColor: '#f0f4f8',
                targetFontSize: 60, // 默认值，resize 中会更新
                particleSizeMin: 2.8,
                particleSizeMax: 5.2,
                fadeStart: 0.30,
                fadeEnd: 0.68,
                particlesPerChar: 400,
                charGap: 30, // 默认值，resize 中会更新
                jitterMax: 5000,
                stableParticleAlpha: 0.7,
            };

            function resize() {
                W = canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
                H = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
                if (isNaN(H) || H <= 0) H = window.innerHeight || 600;

                // === 移动端适配：更新 PARTICLE_CFG 目标字体大小 ===
                const base = 110;
                const minSz = 36;
                const maxSz = 110;
                const ratio = Math.min(W / 768, 1);
                let sz = base * (0.6 + 0.4 * ratio);
                sz = Math.max(minSz, Math.min(maxSz, sz));
                PARTICLE_CFG.targetFontSize = sz;
                PARTICLE_CFG.charGap = sz * 0.45;
            }
            window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
            resize();

            // ================================================================
            // 声音系统
            // ================================================================
            let soundSystem = null;
            let soundEngine = null;
            let meteorManager = null;
            let fireworkManager = null;
            let soundInitialized = false;
            let soundMuted = false;
            let currentCitySoundId = null;
            let currentSpaceSoundId = null;
            let heartbeatSoundId = null;
            let birthdaySoundId = null;

            // ★ 用于存储流星雨音效 ID 和单体陨星音效 ID
            let activeShowerSoundIds = [];
            let meteorSoundId = null;

            soundToggle.addEventListener('click', async () => {
                if (!soundInitialized) {
                    await initSoundSystem();
                }
                soundMuted = !soundMuted;
                if (soundEngine) {
                    soundEngine.setMuted(soundMuted);
                    if (soundMuted) birthdaySoundId = null;
                }
                soundToggle.textContent = soundMuted ? '🔇 音效已静音' : '🔊 音效已开启';
                soundToggle.classList.add('show');
                if (!soundMuted) {
                    resumeSoundForCurrentPhase();
                }
            });

            async function initSoundSystem() {
                if (soundInitialized) return;
                try {
                    soundSystem = await SoundSystem.create();
                    soundEngine = soundSystem.engine;
                    meteorManager = soundSystem.meteorManager;
                    fireworkManager = soundSystem.fireworkManager;
                    soundInitialized = true;
                    soundMuted = false;
                    soundEngine.setMuted(false);
                    soundToggle.textContent = '🔊 音效已开启';
                    soundToggle.classList.add('show');
                    console.log('✅ 声音系统初始化成功');
                } catch (err) {
                    console.warn('⚠️ 声音系统初始化失败:', err);
                    soundToggle.textContent = '❌ 音效不可用';
                    soundToggle.classList.add('show');
                    throw err;
                }
            }

            function resumeSoundForCurrentPhase() {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted) return;
                switch (currentPhase) {
                    case MASTER_PHASE.EPIC:
                        playEpicSoundByState(epicFlowState);
                        break;
                    case MASTER_PHASE.FIREWORKS:
                        break;
                    case MASTER_PHASE.CAKE_TRANSITION:
                    case MASTER_PHASE.CAKE_FLOW:
                        playBirthdayMusic();
                        break;
                    default:
                        break;
                }
            }

            // --- 背景氛围音效 (全部改为循环 + ignoreEndTime) ---
            async function playCityAmbient() {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted) return;
                if (currentCitySoundId && soundEngine.isPlaying(currentCitySoundId)) return;
                if (currentCitySoundId) soundEngine.stopSound(currentCitySoundId);
                const entry = await soundEngine.playSound('city', 0, 6, 0.35, 0, true, true);
                if (entry) currentCitySoundId = entry.id;
            }

            async function playSpaceAmbient() {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted) return;
                if (currentSpaceSoundId && soundEngine.isPlaying(currentSpaceSoundId)) return;
                if (currentSpaceSoundId) soundEngine.stopSound(currentSpaceSoundId);
                const entry = await soundEngine.playSound('space', 0, 4, 0.30, 0, true, true);
                if (entry) currentSpaceSoundId = entry.id;
            }

            async function playHeartbeat() {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted) return;
                if (heartbeatSoundId && soundEngine.isPlaying(heartbeatSoundId)) return;
                if (heartbeatSoundId) soundEngine.stopSound(heartbeatSoundId);
                const entry = await soundEngine.playSound('heartbeat', 0, 2, 0.35, 0, false, false);
                if (entry) heartbeatSoundId = entry.id;
                setTimeout(() => {
                    if (heartbeatSoundId) {
                        soundEngine.stopSound(heartbeatSoundId);
                        heartbeatSoundId = null;
                    }
                }, 2500);
            }

            function stopHeartbeat() {
                if (heartbeatSoundId) {
                    soundEngine.stopSound(heartbeatSoundId);
                    heartbeatSoundId = null;
                }
            }

            function stopCityAmbient() {
                if (currentCitySoundId) {
                    soundEngine.stopSound(currentCitySoundId);
                    currentCitySoundId = null;
                }
            }

            function stopSpaceAmbient() {
                if (currentSpaceSoundId) {
                    soundEngine.stopSound(currentSpaceSoundId);
                    currentSpaceSoundId = null;
                }
            }

            function stopAllAmbient() {
                stopCityAmbient();
                stopSpaceAmbient();
                stopHeartbeat();
            }

            // --- Birthday 音乐 (循环 + ignoreEndTime) ---
            async function playBirthdayMusic() {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted || !soundEngine) return;
                if (birthdaySoundId && soundEngine.isPlaying(birthdaySoundId)) return;
                if (birthdaySoundId) {
                    soundEngine.stopSound(birthdaySoundId);
                    birthdaySoundId = null;
                }
                const cfg = soundSystem.config.sounds.birthday;
                const entry = await soundEngine.playSound('birthday', cfg?.start || 0, cfg?.end || 12, 0.55, 0, true, true);
                if (entry) {
                    birthdaySoundId = entry.id;
                    console.log('🎂 Birthday 音乐已开始循环播放 (完整音频)');
                }
            }

            function stopBirthdayMusic() {
                if (birthdaySoundId) {
                    soundEngine.stopSound(birthdaySoundId);
                    birthdaySoundId = null;
                    console.log('🎂 Birthday 音乐已停止');
                }
            }

            // --- 短音效 (流星雨单颗流星：播放完整音频) ---
            async function playShowerMeteorSound(pan, volume) {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted || !soundEngine) return null;
                const entry = await soundEngine.playSound('meteor', 0, 1.5, volume || 0.5, pan || 0, false, true);
                if (entry) {
                    activeShowerSoundIds.push(entry.id);
                    entry.source.onended = () => {
                        const idx = activeShowerSoundIds.indexOf(entry.id);
                        if (idx > -1) activeShowerSoundIds.splice(idx, 1);
                    };
                    return entry;
                }
                return null;
            }

            // --- 烟花音效 ---
            async function playFireworkLaunchSound(pan, volume) {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted || !soundEngine) return;
                await soundEngine.playSound('firework_launch', 0, 1.8, volume || 0.7, pan || 0, false, false);
            }

            async function playFireworkExplodeSound(pan, volume) {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted || !soundEngine) return;
                await soundEngine.playSound('firework_explode', 0, 2.5, volume || 0.9, pan || 0, false, false);
            }

            // --- 蛋糕音效 ---
            function playCakeSound() {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted) return;
                playBirthdayMusic();
                playHeartbeat();
            }

            // ★ 工具函数：停止所有流星雨音效和单体陨星音效
            function stopAllMeteorSounds() {
                if (!soundEngine) return;
                activeShowerSoundIds.forEach(id => soundEngine.stopSound(id));
                activeShowerSoundIds = [];
                if (meteorSoundId) {
                    soundEngine.stopSound(meteorSoundId);
                    meteorSoundId = null;
                }
                console.log('🔇 已停止所有流星雨音效');
            }

            // --- Epic 状态音效 ---
            function playEpicSoundByState(state) {
                if (!soundInitialized || soundMuted) return;
                switch (state) {
                    case 'city_open':
                    case 'city_rise':
                        playCityAmbient();
                        stopSpaceAmbient();
                        stopHeartbeat();
                        break;
                    case 'city_fade':
                        playCityAmbient();
                        playSpaceAmbient();
                        break;
                    case 'space_open':
                    case 'attached':
                        stopCityAmbient();
                        playSpaceAmbient();
                        playHeartbeat();
                        break;
                    case 'flying':
                    case 'broken':
                        stopCityAmbient();
                        playSpaceAmbient();
                        break;
                    case 'meteor_shower':
                    case 'ended':
                        stopCityAmbient();
                        stopHeartbeat();
                        playSpaceAmbient();
                        break;
                    case 'retreat':
                        stopCityAmbient();
                        playSpaceAmbient();
                        break;
                    default:
                        break;
                }
            }

            // ================================================================
            // 主阶段管理
            // ================================================================
            const MASTER_PHASE = {
                EPIC: 'epic',
                CLOSING: 'closing',
                FIREWORKS: 'fireworks',
                CAKE_TRANSITION: 'cake_transition',
                CAKE_FLOW: 'cake_flow'
            };

            let currentPhase = MASTER_PHASE.EPIC;
            let phaseStartTime = 0;
            let animId = null;
            let fireworksStartTime = 0;
            let fireworksPhaseReady = false;
            let cakeTransitionProgress = 0;
            let cakeFlowInitialized = false;
            let cakeFlowStarted = false;

            // ================================================================
            // 阶段一：Epic (视觉部分)
            // ================================================================
            const CONFIG = {
                eye: { duration: 2000 },
                rise: { duration: 4000, maxOffsetY: 1.2 },
                fade: { duration: 3000, cityDrop: 1.2, viewportFollow: 0.08, startScale: 1.0, endScale: 1.0 },
                spaceOpen: { duration: 4000, startScale: 1.0, endScale: 1.25 },
                starAppear: { start: 0.15, end: 1, fadeRange: 0.06, randomOffset: 0.06, atmosphereStrength: 0.5 },
                starFlicker: { speedMin: 0.2, speedMax: 0.8, amplitudeMin: 0.15, amplitudeMax: 0.35 },
                meteor: {
                    attachDuration: 2000,
                    startSpeed: 4,
                    maxSpeed: 15,
                    startBlur: 15,
                    maxBlur: 55,
                    historyLength: 22,
                    gravity: 0.06,
                    flyDurationMin: 50,
                    flyDurationMax: 80,
                    pieceCountMin: 8,
                    pieceCountMax: 13,
                    pieceHistoryLen: 12,
                },
                shower: {
                    maxCount: 20,
                    angleMin: 0.55,
                    angleMax: 0.85,
                    speedMin: 9,
                    speedMax: 16,
                    tailMin: 200,
                    tailMax: 400,
                    lifeMin: 60,
                    lifeMax: 100,
                    spawnIntervalMin: 300,
                    spawnIntervalMax: 800,
                    duration: 15000,
                    trailSegments: 60,
                    baseWidthMin: 4.0,
                    baseWidthMax: 7.0,
                    brightnessMin: 0.8,
                    brightnessMax: 1.0,
                    fadeOutStart: 0.12,
                },
                glow: {
                    intensity: 0.7,
                    farAlpha: 0.06,
                    midAlpha: 0.12,
                    nearAlpha: 0.20,
                    farRadius: 0.6,
                    midRadius: 1.0,
                    nearRadius: 1.4,
                    farOffset: 2,
                    midOffset: 4,
                    nearOffset: 8,
                    horizonAlpha: 0.06,
                },
                visual: { vignetteStrength: 0.18, lensGlowStart: 0.6, lensGlowAlpha: 0.04 }
            };

            // --- 城市星星 ---
            const CITY_STAR_COLORS = [
                { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 245, b: 235 },
                { r: 235, g: 240, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 230, b: 210 },
                { r: 210, g: 220, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 210, b: 180 }
            ];
            let cityStars = [];

            function generateCityStars() {
                cityStars = [];
                const count = 280 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 60);
                const { speedMin, speedMax, amplitudeMin, amplitudeMax } = CONFIG.starFlicker;
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    let magnitude = Math.random();
                    let brightness = Math.pow(magnitude, 2.5);
                    brightness = clamp(brightness, 0.05, 1.0);
                    const size = 0.3 + brightness * 2.7;
                    const isBright = brightness > 0.7 && Math.random() < 0.3;
                    let colorIdx;
                    if (brightness > 0.7) colorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
                    else if (brightness > 0.4) colorIdx = 3 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
                    else colorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * CITY_STAR_COLORS.length);
                    const color = CITY_STAR_COLORS[colorIdx % CITY_STAR_COLORS.length];
                    const speed = speedMin + Math.random() * (speedMax - speedMin);
                    const phase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const flickerAmt = amplitudeMin + brightness * (amplitudeMax - amplitudeMin);
                    const x = Math.random() * (W - 20) + 10;
                    const y = Math.random() * (H * 0.75) + 10;
                    const randomDelay = (Math.random() - 0.5) * CONFIG.starAppear.randomOffset * 2;
                    cityStars.push({
                        x,
                        y,
                        size,
                        brightness,
                        baseAlpha: 0.6 + brightness * 0.4,
                        alpha: 0,
                        speed,
                        phase,
                        flickerAmt,
                        color,
                        isBright,
                        randomDelay
                    });
                }
            }
            generateCityStars();

            function updateCityStars(time) {
                for (const star of cityStars) {
                    const flicker = Math.sin(time * star.speed + star.phase);
                    const af = 0.5 + 0.5 * flicker;
                    const mod = 1 + star.flickerAmt * (af - 0.5) * 2;
                    star.alpha = star.baseAlpha * mod;
                    star.alpha = clamp(star.alpha, 0.05, 1);
                }
            }

            function drawCityStars(time, liftProgress, vp, alphaMul) {
                const { start, end, fadeRange, atmosphereStrength } = CONFIG.starAppear;
                if (liftProgress < start - fadeRange * 0.5) return;
                updateCityStars(time);
                const sorted = [...cityStars].sort((a, b) => b.brightness - a.brightness);
                const scale = vp ? vp.scale : 1.0;
                const ox = vp ? (W / 2 - vp.cx) * scale + W / 2 : W / 2;
                const oy = vp ? (H / 2 - vp.cy) * scale + H / 2 : H / 2;
                const alphaMult = (alphaMul !== undefined) ? alphaMul : 1.0;
                for (const star of sorted) {
                    const yNorm = star.y / (H * 0.75);
                    const baseThreshold = start + (end - start) * yNorm;
                    const threshold = clamp(baseThreshold + star.randomDelay, start - 0.05, end + 0.05);
                    const fadeEnd2 = threshold + fadeRange;
                    let visibility = smoothstep(threshold, fadeEnd2, liftProgress);
                    if (liftProgress > end + fadeRange) visibility = 1;
                    const heightFactor = 1 - yNorm;
                    const atmosphere = (1 - atmosphereStrength) + atmosphereStrength * heightFactor;
                    const sizeFactor = 0.6 + 0.4 * heightFactor;
                    const finalAlpha = star.alpha * visibility * atmosphere * alphaMult;
                    if (finalAlpha < 0.001) continue;
                    const px = (star.x - W / 2) * scale + ox;
                    const py = (star.y - H / 2) * scale + oy;
                    const finalSize = star.size * sizeFactor * scale;
                    const { color, isBright } = star;
                    const { r, g, b } = color;
                    if (finalSize > 0.8 && finalAlpha > 0.02) {
                        const glowR = finalSize * 2.5;
                        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(px, py, 0, px, py, glowR);
                        grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.35})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.10})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},0)`);
                        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(px, py, glowR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fill();
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha;
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(px, py, finalSize * 0.85, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                    if (isBright && finalAlpha > 0.2) {
                        const len = 10 + finalSize * 4;
                        for (let ang = 0; ang < Math.PI * 2; ang += Math.PI / 4) {
                            const ca = Math.cos(ang),
                                sa = Math.sin(ang);
                            ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha * 0.25;
                            ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.25})`;
                            ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.moveTo(px + ca * 1.5, py + sa * 1.5);
                            ctx.lineTo(px + ca * len, py + sa * len);
                            ctx.stroke();
                        }
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                }
            }

            // --- 太空星星 ---
            const SPACE_STAR_COLORS = [
                { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 245, b: 235 },
                { r: 235, g: 240, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 230, b: 210 },
                { r: 210, g: 220, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 210, b: 180 }
            ];
            const SPACE_LAYERS = [
                { name: '远层', parallax: 0.15, count: 300, sizeMin: 0.2, sizeMax: 1.2, alphaBase: 0.3 },
                { name: '中层', parallax: 0.45, count: 250, sizeMin: 0.4, sizeMax: 2.0, alphaBase: 0.5 },
                { name: '近层', parallax: 0.95, count: 150, sizeMin: 0.8, sizeMax: 3.5, alphaBase: 0.7 }
            ];
            let spaceStarLayers = [];

            function initSpaceStars() {
                spaceStarLayers = [];
                for (let li = 0; li < SPACE_LAYERS.length; li++) {
                    const cfg = SPACE_LAYERS[li];
                    const stars = [];
                    for (let i = 0; i < cfg.count; i++) {
                        const mag = Math.random(),
                            bri = Math.pow(mag, 1.8);
                        const size = cfg.sizeMin + bri * (cfg.sizeMax - cfg.sizeMin);
                        const isBright = bri > 0.7 && Math.random() < 0.2;
                        const ci = Math.floor(Math.random() * SPACE_STAR_COLORS.length);
                        const col = SPACE_STAR_COLORS[ci];
                        const spd = 0.15 + Math.random() * 0.6,
                            ph = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                        const fa = 0.1 + bri * 0.3;
                        stars.push({
                            x: Math.random() * W,
                            y: Math.random() * H,
                            size,
                            brightness: bri,
                            baseAlpha: cfg.alphaBase + bri * 0.5,
                            alpha: 0,
                            speed: spd,
                            phase: ph,
                            flickerAmt: fa,
                            color: col,
                            isBright,
                            parallax: cfg.parallax
                        });
                    }
                    spaceStarLayers.push(stars);
                }
            }
            initSpaceStars();

            function drawSpaceStars(time, vp, alphaMul) {
                const scale = vp.scale;
                const alphaMult = (alphaMul !== undefined) ? alphaMul : 1.0;
                if (alphaMult < 0.001) return;
                for (let li = 0; li < spaceStarLayers.length; li++) {
                    const stars = spaceStarLayers[li];
                    const parallax = SPACE_LAYERS[li].parallax;
                    const offFactor = (scale - 0.05) * 0.8;
                    const offX = (vp.cx - W / 2) * parallax * offFactor * 0.3;
                    const offY = (vp.cy - H / 2) * parallax * offFactor * 0.3;
                    for (const star of stars) {
                        const flicker = Math.sin(time * star.speed + star.phase);
                        const af = 0.5 + 0.5 * flicker;
                        const mod = 1 + star.flickerAmt * (af - 0.5) * 2;
                        star.alpha = star.baseAlpha * mod;
                        star.alpha = clamp(star.alpha, 0.05, 1);
                        const px = star.x + offX * (1 + star.parallax * 0.5);
                        const py = star.y + offY * (1 + star.parallax * 0.5);
                        const szScale = 0.8 + 0.4 * (1 - parallax);
                        const drawSize = star.size * szScale;
                        const finalAlpha = star.alpha * alphaMult;
                        if (finalAlpha < 0.001) continue;
                        if (drawSize > 0.8 && finalAlpha > 0.02) {
                            const glowR = drawSize * 2.5;
                            const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(px, py, 0, px, py, glowR);
                            grad.addColorStop(0,
                                `rgba(${star.color.r},${star.color.g},${star.color.b},${finalAlpha * 0.3})`);
                            grad.addColorStop(0.5,
                                `rgba(${star.color.r},${star.color.g},${star.color.b},${finalAlpha * 0.08})`);
                            grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${star.color.r},${star.color.g},${star.color.b},0)`);
                            ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.arc(px, py, glowR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                            ctx.fill();
                        }
                        ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha;
                        ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${star.color.r},${star.color.g},${star.color.b})`;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(px, py, drawSize * 0.85, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fill();
                        if (star.isBright && finalAlpha > 0.2) {
                            const len = 10 + drawSize * 4;
                            for (let ang = 0; ang < Math.PI * 2; ang += Math.PI / 4) {
                                const ca = Math.cos(ang),
                                    sa = Math.sin(ang);
                                ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha * 0.2;
                                ctx.strokeStyle =
                                    `rgba(${star.color.r},${star.color.g},${star.color.b},${finalAlpha * 0.2})`;
                                ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
                                ctx.beginPath();
                                ctx.moveTo(px + ca * 1.5, py + sa * 1.5);
                                ctx.lineTo(px + ca * len, py + sa * len);
                                ctx.stroke();
                            }
                        }
                        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                    }
                }
            }

            // --- 悬浮粒子 ---
            let floatParticles = [];
            const PARTICLE_COUNT = 120;

            function initFloatParticles() {
                floatParticles = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < PARTICLE_COUNT; i++) {
                    floatParticles.push({
                        x: rand(0, W),
                        y: rand(0, H),
                        z: rand(0.1, 1.0),
                        size: rand(0.5, 2.8),
                        vx: rand(-0.15, 0.15),
                        vy: rand(-0.10, 0.10),
                        phase: rand(0, Math.PI * 2),
                        alpha: rand(0.05, 0.25),
                        color: { r: rand(180, 255), g: rand(180, 255), b: rand(200, 255) }
                    });
                }
            }
            initFloatParticles();

            function updateFloatParticles(time) {
                for (const p of floatParticles) {
                    p.x += p.vx + Math.sin(time * 0.0003 + p.phase) * 0.04;
                    p.y += p.vy + Math.cos(time * 0.0004 + p.phase * 1.3) * 0.03;
                    if (p.x < -20) p.x = W + 20;
                    if (p.x > W + 20) p.x = -20;
                    if (p.y < -20) p.y = H + 20;
                    if (p.y > H + 20) p.y = -20;
                    p.alpha = p.alpha * 0.998 + (0.05 + 0.2 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(time * 0.001 + p.phase))) * 0.002;
                    p.alpha = clamp(p.alpha, 0.02, 0.35);
                }
            }

            function drawFloatParticles() {
                for (const p of floatParticles) {
                    const depth = p.z,
                        size = p.size * (0.4 + 0.6 * depth),
                        alpha = p.alpha * (0.3 + 0.7 * depth);
                    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${p.color.r},${p.color.g},${p.color.b},${alpha})`;
                    ctx.fill();
                    if (size > 0.8 && alpha > 0.05) {
                        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x, p.y, 0, p.x, p.y, size * 3);
                        grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${p.color.r},${p.color.g},${p.color.b},${alpha * 0.2})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${p.color.r},${p.color.g},${p.color.b},0)`);
                        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, size * 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fill();
                    }
                }
                ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
            }

            // --- 星云 ---
            let clouds = [];

            function initClouds() {
                clouds = [];
                const count = 30 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30);
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    const cx = Math.random() * W,
                        cy = Math.random() * H * 0.7;
                    const radius = 80 + Math.random() * 300;
                    const alpha = 0.015 + Math.random() * 0.035;
                    let r, g, b;
                    const s = Math.random();
                    if (s < 0.33) {
                        r = 80;
                        g = 120;
                        b = 200;
                    } else if (s < 0.66) {
                        r = 120;
                        g = 100;
                        b = 180;
                    } else {
                        r = 200;
                        g = 180;
                        b = 160;
                    }
                    clouds.push({ x: cx, y: cy, radius, alpha, r, g, b });
                }
            }
            initClouds();

            function drawClouds(scale) {
                if (clouds.length === 0) return;
                const ca = smoothstep(0.05, 0.6, scale);
                ctx.save();
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                for (const cloud of clouds) {
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cloud.x, cloud.y, 0, cloud.x, cloud.y, cloud.radius);
                    const a = cloud.alpha * (0.3 + 0.7 * (1 - ca));
                    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${cloud.r},${cloud.g},${cloud.b},${a})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(${cloud.r},${cloud.g},${cloud.b},${a * 0.5})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${cloud.r},${cloud.g},${cloud.b},0)`);
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(cloud.x, cloud.y, cloud.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                }
                ctx.restore();
            }

            // --- 建筑系统 ---
            let buildings = [];

            function generateBuildings() {
                buildings = [];
                const horizonY = H * 0.20;

                function perspective(baseY) {
                    let t = (baseY - horizonY) / (H * 0.85 - horizonY);
                    return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
                }

                function fillRow(yMin, yMax, cellW, layerType) {
                    const rows = Math.max(1, Math.round((yMax - yMin) / (H * 0.045)));
                    const rowH = (yMax - yMin) / rows;
                    const phase = Math.random() * cellW;
                    const cols = Math.ceil(W / cellW) + 1;
                    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
                        const rowOff = phase + (r % 2 === 1 ? cellW * 0.5 : 0) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * cellW * 0.3;
                        let prevBh = -1;
                        for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
                            if (Math.random() < 0.08) continue;
                            const baseY = yMin + r * rowH + Math.random() * rowH;
                            const t = perspective(baseY);
                            let bw, bh;
                            if (layerType === 'far') {
                                bw = 5 + Math.random() * 5 + t * 6;
                                const tf = 1 - t;
                                bh = (13 + Math.random() * 14) * (0.60 + tf * 1.15);
                                if (Math.random() < 0.07) bh *= 1.5 + Math.random() * 0.6;
                            } else if (layerType === 'mid') {
                                bw = 14 + Math.random() * 14 + t * 14;
                                bh = (30 + Math.random() * 26) * (0.45 + t * 1.1);
                            } else {
                                bw = 34 + Math.random() * 26 + t * 24;
                                bh = (60 + Math.random() * 46) * (0.5 + t * 0.9);
                            }
                            if (prevBh > 0 && Math.abs(bh - prevBh) < prevBh * 0.12) bh *= Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1.25 :
                                0.78;
                            prevBh = bh;
                            bw = Math.min(bw, cellW * 0.85);
                            const jit = (Math.random() - 0.5) * cellW * 0.5;
                            const bx = c * cellW + rowOff + jit;
                            if (bx + bw < 0 || bx > W) continue;
                            const seed = hash(bx, baseY);
                            const windows = [];
                            if (layerType === 'far') {
                                const cnt = 3 + Math.floor(seed * 6);
                                for (let i = 0; i < cnt; i++) {
                                    windows.push({
                                        x: 1 + Math.random() * (bw - 2),
                                        y: 1 + Math.random() * (bh - 2),
                                        w: 1,
                                        h: Math.random() > 0.5 ? 2 : 1,
                                        lit: true,
                                        alpha: 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.4
                                    });
                                }
                            } else {
                                const scale = (layerType === 'mid') ? (0.8 + t * 0.4) : (0.85 + t * 0.35);
                                const winW = (layerType === 'mid') ? 3 * scale : 5 * scale;
                                const winH = (layerType === 'mid') ? 5 * scale : 7 * scale;
                                const gapX = (layerType === 'mid') ? 4 : 3,
                                    gapY = (layerType === 'mid') ? 4 : 3;
                                const padX = (layerType === 'mid') ? 4 : 5,
                                    padY = (layerType === 'mid') ? 6 : 8;
                                const stepX = winW + gapX,
                                    stepY = winH + gapY;
                                const colsW = Math.max(1, Math.floor((bw - 2 * padX + gapX) / stepX));
                                const rowsW = Math.max(1, Math.floor((bh - 2 * padY + gapY) / stepY));
                                const totalW = colsW * stepX - gapX,
                                    totalH = rowsW * stepY - gapY;
                                const startX = padX + (bw - 2 * padX - totalW) / 2;
                                const startY = padY + (bh - 2 * padY - totalH) / 2;
                                const rowStates = [];
                                let cr = 0;
                                while (cr < rowsW) {
                                    const lc = 3 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
                                        dc = 2 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
                                    for (let i = 0; i < lc && cr < rowsW; i++) {
                                        rowStates.push(true);
                                        cr++;
                                    }
                                    for (let i = 0; i < dc && cr < rowsW; i++) {
                                        rowStates.push(false);
                                        cr++;
                                    }
                                }
                                const lightRate = (layerType === 'mid') ? (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.2) : (0.35 + Math
                                    .random() *
                                    0.15);
                                for (let r2 = 0; r2 < rowsW; r2++) {
                                    if (!rowStates[r2]) continue;
                                    for (let c2 = 0; c2 < colsW; c2++) {
                                        if (Math.random() > lightRate) continue;
                                        const wx = startX + c2 * stepX,
                                            wy = startY + r2 * stepY;
                                        const rnd = Math.random();
                                        let color;
                                        if (rnd < 0.02) color = '248,113,113';
                                        else if (rnd < 0.07) color = '122,183,212';
                                        else color = Math.random() > 0.5 ? '251,191,36' : '252,211,77';
                                        let alpha = (layerType === 'mid') ? (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7) : (0.7 + Math
                                            .random() * 0.3);
                                        windows.push({ x: wx, y: wy, w: winW, h: winH, lit: true, color, alpha });
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            buildings.push({ type: layerType, x: bx, y: baseY - bh, w: bw, h: bh, t, windows, seed });
                        }
                    }
                }
                fillRow(H * 0.20, H * 0.38, 14, 'far');
                fillRow(H * 0.30, H * 0.62, 34, 'mid');
                fillRow(H * 0.52, H * 0.98, 78, 'near');
            }
            generateBuildings();

            function drawBuilding(b, offY) {
                const { type, x, y, w, h, t, windows } = b;
                const by = y + offY;
                const fog = type === 'far' ? 0.55 : (type === 'mid' ? 0.30 : 0.10);
                const depth = 0.35 + t * 0.65;
                let baseR, baseG, baseB;
                if (type === 'far') {
                    baseR = 18 + t * 10;
                    baseG = baseR + 8;
                    baseB = baseR + 22;
                } else if (type === 'mid') {
                    baseR = (20 + Math.random() * 8) * depth + fog * 20;
                    baseG = (32 + Math.random() * 10) * depth + fog * 26;
                    baseB = (52 + Math.random() * 14) * depth + fog * 38;
                } else {
                    baseR = (13 * depth) | 0;
                    baseG = (22 * depth + 6) | 0;
                    baseB = (36 * depth + 8) | 0;
                }
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${baseR | 0},${baseG | 0},${baseB | 0})`;
                ctx.fillRect(x, by, w, h);
                const roofH = Math.max(2, h * (type === 'far' ? 0.03 : 0.06));
                const roofR = Math.min(255, baseR + 25),
                    roofG = Math.min(255, baseG + 30),
                    roofB = Math.min(255, baseB + 35);
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${roofR | 0},${roofG | 0},${roofB | 0})`;
                ctx.fillRect(x, by - roofH, w, roofH);
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(200,220,255,${0.05 + t * 0.08})`;
                ctx.fillRect(x, by - roofH, w, 0.8);
                const sideW = Math.max(2, w * 0.08);
                ctx.fillStyle =
                    `rgb(${Math.max(0, baseR * 0.6) | 0},${Math.max(0, baseG * 0.6) | 0},${Math.max(0, baseB * 0.6) | 0})`;
                ctx.fillRect(x + w, by, sideW, h);
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(150,180,220,${0.03 + t * 0.06})`;
                ctx.fillRect(x + w + sideW - 1, by, 1, h);
                for (const win of windows) {
                    if (!win.lit) continue;
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${win.color || '251,191,36'}, ${win.alpha || 0.2})`;
                    ctx.fillRect(x + win.x, by + win.y, win.w, win.h);
                }
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(160,200,240,${0.08 + t * 0.15})`;
                ctx.fillRect(x, by, w, 1.2);
                if (type !== 'far' && Math.random() > 0.5) {
                    const ah = 4 + Math.random() * 10,
                        ax = x + Math.random() * w;
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(160,200,240,${0.05 + t * 0.12})`;
                    ctx.fillRect(ax, by - roofH - ah, 1.0, ah);
