                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,240,200,${0.03 + Math.random() * 0.06})`;
                    ctx.fillRect(ax - 0.5, by - roofH - ah - 1, 2, 1.5);
                }
            }

            function drawCityGlow(offY, intensity, liftProg) {
                if (intensity < 0.01) return;
                const g = CONFIG.glow;
                const nearB = [],
                    midB = [],
                    farB = [];
                buildings.forEach(b => {
                    if (b.type === 'near') nearB.push(b);
                    else if (b.type === 'mid') midB.push(b);
                    else farB.push(b);
                });
                const drawBatch = (batch, alphaMul, radiusMul, off) => {
                    if (batch.length === 0) return;
                    const isNear = batch[0].type === 'near';
                    for (const b of batch) {
                        const t = b.t || 0.5,
                            cx = b.x + b.w / 2,
                            cy = b.y + offY;
                        const sz = Math.max(b.w * 0.4, b.h * 0.15);
                        const radius = sz * (radiusMul + t * 0.5) + off;
                        const alpha = alphaMul * (0.5 + t * 0.5) * intensity * (1 - liftProg * 0.3);
                        if (alpha < 0.002) continue;
                        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, radius);
                        grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,180,60,${alpha * 0.9})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(0.1, `rgba(250,175,55,${alpha * 0.7})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(0.3, `rgba(240,165,50,${alpha * 0.35})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(0.6, `rgba(225,155,45,${alpha * 0.12})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(205,145,40,0)`);
                        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(cx, cy, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fill();
                        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                        if (isNear && alpha > 0.08) {
                            const cr = radius * 0.1,
                                cg = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, cr);
                            cg.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,230,180,${alpha * 0.4})`);
                            cg.addColorStop(1, `rgba(255,200,140,0)`);
                            ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                            ctx.fillStyle = cg;
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.arc(cx, cy, cr, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                            ctx.fill();
                            ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                        }
                    }
                };
                drawBatch(farB, g.farAlpha, g.farRadius, g.farOffset);
                drawBatch(midB, g.midAlpha, g.midRadius, g.midOffset);
                drawBatch(nearB, g.nearAlpha, g.nearRadius, g.nearOffset);
                const ha = g.horizonAlpha * intensity * (1 - liftProg * 0.6);
                if (ha > 0.001) {
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H * 0.75 + offY * 0.5, 0, W / 2, H * 0.75 + offY * 0.5,
                        Math.max(W, H) * 0.5);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,200,150,${ha * 0.8})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(200,160,120,${ha * 0.3})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(100,80,60,0)`);
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(W / 2, H * 0.75 + offY * 0.5, Math.max(W, H) * 0.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                }
            }

            // --- 行星系统 ---
            class Planet {
                constructor(x, y, radius, color, ringLayers, craterCount) {
                    this.x = x;
                    this.y = y;
                    this.radius = radius;
                    this.color = color;
                    this.ringLayers = ringLayers;
                    this.craterCount = craterCount || 20;
                    this.rotation = 0;
                    this.origRadius = radius;
                    this.craters = [];
                    for (let i = 0; i < this.craterCount; i++) {
                        const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
                            d = Math.random() * this.radius * 0.85;
                        const cx = Math.cos(a) * d,
                            cy = Math.sin(a) * d,
                            r = 1.5 + Math.random() * 6,
                            dep = 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7;
                        this.craters.push({ x: cx, y: cy, r, depth: dep });
                    }
                }
                update() { this.rotation += 0.001; for (const l of this.ringLayers) l.angle += l.speed; }
                draw(ctx, cx, cy, radiusOverride, alpha, blurAmt) {
                    const px = cx !== undefined ? cx : this.x,
                        py = cy !== undefined ? cy : this.y;
                    const r = radiusOverride !== undefined ? radiusOverride : this.radius;
                    if (blurAmt && blurAmt > 0.5) {
                        ctx.shadowBlur = blurAmt * 4;
                        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0)';
                    }
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(px - r * 0.3, py - r * 0.3, r * 0.1, px, py, r);
                    const base = this.color;
                    grad.addColorStop(0, lighten(base, 40));
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, base);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, darken(base, 60));
                    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(px, py, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(180,200,255,0.12)';
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 35;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(px, py, r * 0.98, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,220,255,0.04)';
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    ctx.save();
                    ctx.translate(px, py);
                    ctx.rotate(this.rotation);
                    const sf = r / this.origRadius;
                    for (const c of this.craters) {
                        const cr = c.r * sf;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.ellipse(c.x * sf, c.y * sf, cr * 0.8, cr * 0.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${0.15 + c.depth * 0.2})`;
                        ctx.fill();
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.ellipse(c.x * sf - cr * 0.1, c.y * sf - cr * 0.1, cr * 0.7, cr * 0.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,240,${0.05 + c.depth * 0.1})`;
                        ctx.fill();
                        if (c.depth > 0.6) {
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.arc(c.x * sf - cr * 0.3, c.y * sf - cr * 0.3, cr * 0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)';
                            ctx.fill();
                        }
                    }
                    ctx.restore();
                    ctx.save();
                    ctx.translate(px, py);
                    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                        const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
                            d = r * (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.6);
                        const x = Math.cos(a) * d,
                            y = Math.sin(a) * d;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(x, y, 2 + Math.random() * 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,240,${0.03 + Math.random() * 0.08})`;
                        ctx.fill();
                    }
                    ctx.restore();
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                }
                drawRings(ctx, cx, cy, radiusOverride, alpha, blurAmt) {
                    const px = cx !== undefined ? cx : this.x,
                        py = cy !== undefined ? cy : this.y;
                    const r = radiusOverride !== undefined ? radiusOverride : this.radius;
                    const sf = r / this.origRadius;
                    for (const layer of this.ringLayers) {
                        this.drawRingLayer(ctx, layer, px, py, sf, alpha, blurAmt);
                    }
                }
                drawRingLayer(ctx, layer, px, py, sf, alpha, blurAmt) {
                    const { particles, a, b, tilt, angle, color } = layer;
                    const cosT = Math.cos(tilt),
                        sinT = Math.sin(tilt);
                    const aS = a * sf,
                        bS = b * sf;
                    if (blurAmt && blurAmt > 0.5) {
                        ctx.shadowBlur = blurAmt * 3;
                        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0)';
                    }
                    for (const p of particles) {
                        if (p.detached) continue;
                        const totalA = p.angleOffset + angle;
                        const rx = aS + p.radialOffset * sf,
                            ry = bS + p.radialOffset * sf * 0.6;
                        const x0 = rx * Math.cos(totalA),
                            y0 = ry * Math.sin(totalA);
                        const lx = x0 * cosT - y0 * sinT,
                            ly = x0 * sinT + y0 * cosT;
                        const wx = px + lx,
                            wy = py + ly;
                        const bri = 0.6 + 0.4 * (1 - Math.hypot(lx, ly) / (aS * 1.2));
                        const sz = p.size * sf * 0.9;
                        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * (0.6 + 0.4 * bri);
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(wx, wy, sz, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fillStyle = color;
                        ctx.fill();
                        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 3;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(wx - sz * 0.2, wy - sz * 0.2, sz * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
                        ctx.fill();
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                }
                getParticlePosition(li, pi) {
                    const layer = this.ringLayers[li],
                        p = layer.particles[pi];
                    const totalA = p.angleOffset + layer.angle;
                    const rx = layer.a + p.radialOffset,
                        ry = layer.b + p.radialOffset * 0.6;
                    const x0 = rx * Math.cos(totalA),
                        y0 = ry * Math.sin(totalA);
                    const cosT = Math.cos(layer.tilt),
                        sinT = Math.sin(layer.tilt);
                    const lx = x0 * cosT - y0 * sinT,
                        ly = x0 * sinT + y0 * cosT;
                    return { x: this.x + lx, y: this.y + ly };
                }
                getParticleTangent(li, pi) {
                    const layer = this.ringLayers[li],
                        p = layer.particles[pi];
                    const totalA = p.angleOffset + layer.angle;
                    const rx = layer.a + p.radialOffset,
                        ry = layer.b + p.radialOffset * 0.6;
                    const tx = -rx * Math.sin(totalA),
                        ty = ry * Math.cos(totalA);
                    const cosT = Math.cos(layer.tilt),
                        sinT = Math.sin(layer.tilt);
                    const ltx = tx * cosT - ty * sinT,
                        lty = tx * sinT + ty * cosT;
                    return Math.atan2(lty, ltx);
                }
                detachParticle(li, pi) { this.ringLayers[li].particles[pi].detached = true; }
                resetAllParticles() {
                    for (const l of this.ringLayers)
                        for (const p of l.particles) p.detached = false;
                }
            }

            function createRingParticles(count, a, b) {
                const ps = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    ps.push({
                        angleOffset: (i / count) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.random() * 0.02,
                        size: 1.8 + Math.random() * 3.2,
                        radialOffset: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 15,
                        detached: false
                    });
                }
                return ps;
            }

            let planets = [],
                mainPlanet = null;
            let attachInfo = { layerIndex: 0, angle: -0.6, x: 0, y: 0, tangentX: 1, tangentY: 0 };

            function initPlanets() {
                planets = [];
                const minDim = Math.min(W, H);
                const pos = [{ x: W * 0.28, y: H * 0.38 }, { x: W * 0.72, y: H * 0.22 }, { x: W * 0.20, y: H * 0.78 },
                { x: W * 0.75, y: H * 0.72 }, { x: W * 0.50, y: H * 0.15 }];
                const cfg = [{ radius: minDim * 0.08, color: '#b8a088', layers: 3, tiltBase: 0.2 },
                { radius: minDim * 0.06, color: '#a09080', layers: 2, tiltBase: 0.8 },
                { radius: minDim * 0.055, color: '#908880', layers: 2, tiltBase: 1.0 },
                { radius: minDim * 0.07, color: '#8a9a7a', layers: 2, tiltBase: 0.5 },
                { radius: minDim * 0.05, color: '#9a8a8a', layers: 1, tiltBase: 1.2 }];
                for (let idx = 0; idx < 5; idx++) {
                    const p = pos[idx],
                        c = cfg[idx];
                    const ringLayers = [];
                    for (let i = 0; i < c.layers; i++) {
                        const a = c.radius * (2.2 + i * 1.2),
                            b = a * (0.5 + Math.random() * 0.25);
                        const tilt = c.tiltBase + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8;
                        const speed = (0.005 + Math.random() * 0.005) * (i + 1) * 0.5;
                        const count = Math.floor(120 + Math.random() * 100);
                        const colors = ['#8a7a6a', '#7a8a7a', '#9a8a7a', '#8a8a7a', '#7a7a7a'];
                        const col = colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];
                        ringLayers.push({
                            particles: createRingParticles(count, a, b),
                            a,
                            b,
                            tilt,
                            angle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
                            speed,
                            color: col
                        });
                    }
                    planets.push(new Planet(p.x, p.y, c.radius, c.color, ringLayers, 25 + Math.floor(Math.random() *
                        20)));
                }
                mainPlanet = planets[0];
                const li = 1,
                    ang = -0.6;
                let best = null,
                    bestDiff = Infinity;
                const layer = mainPlanet.ringLayers[li];
                if (layer) {
                    for (let pi = 0; pi < layer.particles.length; pi++) {
                        const p = layer.particles[pi];
                        const totalA = p.angleOffset + layer.angle;
                        let diff = totalA - ang;
                        diff = ((diff % (2 * Math.PI)) + 3 * Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI) - Math.PI;
                        if (Math.abs(diff) < bestDiff) {
                            bestDiff = Math.abs(diff);
                            best = pi;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (best !== null) {
                    const pos2 = mainPlanet.getParticlePosition(li, best);
                    const tangent = mainPlanet.getParticleTangent(li, best);
                    attachInfo.x = pos2.x;
                    attachInfo.y = pos2.y;
                    attachInfo.layerIndex = li;
                    attachInfo.angle = ang;
                    attachInfo.tangentX = tangent ? Math.cos(tangent) : 1;
                    attachInfo.tangentY = tangent ? Math.sin(tangent) : 0;
                } else {
                    const r = mainPlanet.radius * 2.5;
                    attachInfo.x = mainPlanet.x + r * Math.cos(ang);
                    attachInfo.y = mainPlanet.y + r * Math.sin(ang);
                    attachInfo.tangentX = -Math.sin(ang);
                    attachInfo.tangentY = Math.cos(ang);
                }
                resetMeteor();
            }

            // --- 陨星系统 ---
            let meteor = {
                state: 'attached',
                x: 0,
                y: 0,
                vx: 0,
                vy: 0,
                speed: 0,
                shadowBlur: 0,
                history: [],
                gravity: CONFIG.meteor.gravity,
                brokenPieces: [],
                headColor: { r: 180, g: 160, b: 140 },
                flyTimer: 0,
                flyDuration: 60,
                attachStartTime: 0,
                attachDuration: CONFIG.meteor.attachDuration,
            };

            function resetMeteor() {
                meteor.x = attachInfo.x;
                meteor.y = attachInfo.y;
                meteor.state = 'attached';
                meteor.vx = 0;
                meteor.vy = 0;
                meteor.speed = 0;
                meteor.shadowBlur = 0;
                meteor.history = [];
                meteor.brokenPieces = [];
                meteor.flyTimer = 0;
                meteor.attachStartTime = performance.now();
                meteor.headColor = { r: 180, g: 160, b: 140 };
                if (mainPlanet) mainPlanet.resetAllParticles();
            }

            function triggerDetach() {
                if (meteor.state !== 'attached') return;
                const tx = attachInfo.tangentX,
                    ty = attachInfo.tangentY;
                const dx = tx * 0.7 + 0.3,
                    dy = ty * 0.7 + 0.3;
                const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy),
                    nx = dx / len,
                    ny = dy / len;
                const spd = rand(CONFIG.meteor.startSpeed, CONFIG.meteor.startSpeed + 2);
                meteor.vx = nx * spd;
                meteor.vy = ny * spd;
                meteor.speed = spd;
                meteor.shadowBlur = CONFIG.meteor.startBlur;
                meteor.headColor = { r: 255, g: 200, b: 100 };
                meteor.history = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < CONFIG.meteor.historyLength; i++) {
                    const t = i * 2.2;
                    meteor.history.push({ x: meteor.x - t * nx, y: meteor.y - t * ny });
                }
                meteor.flyTimer = 0;
                meteor.flyDuration = randInt(CONFIG.meteor.flyDurationMin, CONFIG.meteor.flyDurationMax);
                meteor.state = 'flying';
                viewport.targetCx = meteor.x;
                viewport.targetCy = meteor.y;
                viewport.targetScale = CONFIG.spaceOpen.endScale;
                viewport.transitioning = false;
                epicFlowState = 'flying';
                if (soundInitialized && !soundMuted && soundEngine) {
                    soundEngine.playSound('meteor', 0, 3.5, 0.6, 0.2, false, true).then(entry => {
                        if (entry) meteorSoundId = entry.id;
                    });
                }
            }

            function breakMeteor() {
                if (meteor.state !== 'flying' || meteor.brokenPieces.length > 0) return;
                const cx = meteor.x,
                    cy = meteor.y;
                const count = randInt(CONFIG.meteor.pieceCountMin, CONFIG.meteor.pieceCountMax);
                const colors = [
                    { r: 255, g: 200, b: 100 }, { r: 255, g: 160, b: 60 },
                    { r: 240, g: 140, b: 50 }, { r: 255, g: 180, b: 80 },
                    { r: 200, g: 220, b: 255 }
                ];
                const dir = Math.atan2(meteor.vy, meteor.vx),
                    baseSpd = Math.hypot(meteor.vx, meteor.vy) || 3;
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    const off = rand(-Math.PI / 7, Math.PI / 7),
                        a = dir + off,
                        spd = rand(3, 6) + baseSpd * 0.5,
                        sz = rand(5, 16),
                        c = colors[randInt(0, colors.length - 1)];
                    const piece = {
                        x: cx + rand(-6, 6),
                        y: cy + rand(-6, 6),
                        vx: Math.cos(a) * spd,
                        vy: Math.sin(a) * spd - rand(0.3, 1.5),
                        size: sz,
                        color: c,
                        life: randInt(80, 150),
                        alpha: 1,
                        rotation: rand(0, Math.PI * 2),
                        rotSpeed: rand(-0.06, 0.06),
                        history: []
                    };
                    for (let j = 0; j < CONFIG.meteor.pieceHistoryLen; j++) {
                        const t = j * 1.5;
                        piece.history.push({ x: piece.x - Math.cos(a) * t, y: piece.y - Math.sin(a) * t });
                    }
                    meteor.brokenPieces.push(piece);
                }
                meteor.state = 'broken';
                epicFlowState = 'broken';
                if (soundInitialized && !soundMuted && soundEngine) {
                    soundEngine.playSound('meteor', 0, 1.5, 0.4, 0.2, false, true).then(entry => {
                        if (entry) meteorSoundId = entry.id;
                    });
                }
            }

            function drawMeteor() {
                if (meteor.state === 'attached') {
                    const alpha = (epicFlowState === 'space_open' || epicFlowState === 'attached') ? 0.6 : 0.15;
                    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(meteor.x, meteor.y, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#8a7a6a';
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                    return;
                }
                if (meteor.state === 'flying') drawFlyingMeteor();
                else if (meteor.state === 'broken') drawBrokenPieces();
            }

            function drawFlyingMeteor() {
                const hist = meteor.history,
                    len = hist.length;
                if (len === 0) return;
                const hc = meteor.headColor;
                for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
                    const pos = hist[i],
                        t = (i + 1) / len;
                    const alpha = 0.08 + 0.82 * t,
                        radius = 0.8 + 2.8 * t;
                    const col = { r: lerp(hc.r, 255, t), g: lerp(hc.g, 255, t), b: lerp(hc.b, 255, t) };
                    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * 0.85;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(pos.x, pos.y, Math.max(0.3, radius), 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${col.r | 0},${col.g | 0},${col.b | 0})`;
                    ctx.fill();
                    if (t > 0.4) {
                        const ga = (t - 0.4) / 0.6 * 0.15;
                        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 14 * t;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(pos.x, pos.y, radius * 2.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${ga})`;
                        ctx.fill();
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    }
                }
                ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                const last = hist[len - 1];
                if (last) {
                    const blur = meteor.shadowBlur;
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(last.x, last.y, 0, last.x, last.y, Math.max(10, blur * 0.6));
                    const hr = Math.min(255, hc.r + 40),
                        hg = Math.min(255, hc.g + 40),
                        hb = Math.min(255, hc.b + 20);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${hr},${hg},${hb},0.95)`);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.3, `rgba(${hc.r},${hc.g},${hc.b},0.6)`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${hc.r * 0.3 | 0},${hc.g * 0.2 | 0},${hc.b * 0.1 | 0},0)`);
                    ctx.shadowColor = `rgba(${hc.r},${hc.g},${hc.b},0.4)`;
                    ctx.shadowBlur = blur * 0.8;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(last.x, last.y, Math.max(5, blur * 0.6), 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(last.x, last.y, Math.max(2, 3), 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,240,220,0.95)`;
                    ctx.fill();
                }
            }

            function drawBrokenPieces() {
                for (const piece of meteor.brokenPieces) {
                    if (piece.alpha < 0.01) continue;
                    const hist = piece.history,
                        len = hist.length;
                    if (len < 2) continue;
                    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
                        const pos = hist[i],
                            t = (i + 1) / len;
                        const alpha = 0.1 + 0.7 * t,
                            radius = (0.3 + 1.5 * t) * (piece.size / 6);
                        if (radius < 0.1) continue;
                        const c = piece.color,
                            fc = { r: lerp(c.r, 255, t), g: lerp(c.g, 255, t), b: lerp(c.b, 255, t) };
                        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * piece.alpha * 0.6;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(pos.x, pos.y, Math.max(0.2, radius), 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${fc.r | 0},${fc.g | 0},${fc.b | 0})`;
                        ctx.fill();
                        if (t > 0.5) {
                            const ga = (t - 0.5) / 0.5 * 0.08;
                            ctx.shadowColor = `rgba(${c.r},${c.g},${c.b},0.15)`;
                            ctx.shadowBlur = 8 * t;
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.arc(pos.x, pos.y, radius * 2.0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                            ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${c.r},${c.g},${c.b},${ga * piece.alpha})`;
                            ctx.fill();
                            ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                        }
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                }
                for (const piece of meteor.brokenPieces) {
                    const sz = piece.size;
                    if (sz < 0.3 || piece.alpha < 0.01) continue;
                    ctx.globalAlpha = piece.alpha;
                    ctx.save();
                    ctx.translate(piece.x, piece.y);
                    ctx.rotate(piece.rotation);
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(-sz * 0.2, -sz * 0.2, 0, 0, 0, sz);
                    const c = piece.color;
                    grad.addColorStop(0,
                        `rgb(${Math.min(255, c.r + 60)},${Math.min(255, c.g + 40)},${Math.min(255, c.b + 30)})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.6, `rgb(${c.r},${c.g},${c.b})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgb(${c.r * 0.4 | 0},${c.g * 0.3 | 0},${c.b * 0.2 | 0})`);
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    const pts = 5 + randInt(0, 3);
                    for (let i = 0; i < pts; i++) {
                        const a = (i / pts) * Math.PI * 2 + rand(-0.2, 0.2);
                        const r = sz * (0.6 + rand(0, 0.4));
                        if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r);
                        else ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r);
                    }
                    ctx.closePath();
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.globalAlpha = piece.alpha * 0.25;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(-sz * 0.15, -sz * 0.15, sz * 0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.restore();
                }
                ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
            }

            // --- 流星雨 ---
            let showerMeteors = [];
            let showerSpawnTimer = 0;
            let showerActive = false,
                showerGenerating = false,
                showerStartTime = 0,
                showerDuration = CONFIG.shower.duration;

            function getShowerHeadColor() { return { r: rand(235, 255), g: rand(120, 190), b: rand(15, 60) }; }

            function spawnShowerMeteor() {
                const cfg = CONFIG.shower;
                if (showerMeteors.length >= cfg.maxCount) return;
                const angle = rand(cfg.angleMin, cfg.angleMax);
                const speed = rand(cfg.speedMin, cfg.speedMax);
                const vx = Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                    vy = Math.sin(angle) * speed;
                let x, y;
                const side = rand(0, 1);
                if (side < 0.55) {
                    x = rand(-W * 0.1, W * 1.1);
                    y = rand(-80, H * 0.25);
                } else if (side < 0.8) {
                    x = rand(-120, W * 0.15);
                    y = rand(H * 0.05, H * 0.6);
                } else {
                    x = rand(W * 0.85, W + 120);
                    y = rand(H * 0.05, H * 0.5);
                }
                if (x < -50) x = rand(-30, 60);
                if (x > W + 50) x = rand(W - 60, W + 30);
                if (y < -50) y = rand(-20, 40);
                const brightness = rand(cfg.brightnessMin, cfg.brightnessMax);

                const yNorm = clamp(y / H, 0, 1);
                const baseVol = 0.05 + 0.90 * yNorm;
                const xCenterNorm = Math.abs((x / W) - 0.5) * 2;
                const centerBoost = 1 - xCenterNorm * 0.35;
                const randomFactor = 0.75 + 0.50 * Math.random();
                const vol = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.03, baseVol * centerBoost * randomFactor));
                const pan = clamp((x / W) * 2 - 1, -1, 1);

                const meteorObj = {
                    x,
                    y,
                    vx,
                    vy,
                    tailLength: rand(cfg.tailMin, cfg.tailMax),
                    life: randInt(cfg.lifeMin, cfg.lifeMax),
                    maxLife: cfg.lifeMax,
                    brightness: brightness,
                    headColor: getShowerHeadColor(),
                    trailSegments: cfg.trailSegments,
                };
                showerMeteors.push(meteorObj);
                playShowerMeteorSound(pan, vol);
            }

            function updateShowerMeteors() {
                const cfg = CONFIG.shower;
                for (let i = showerMeteors.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                    const m = showerMeteors[i];
                    m.x += m.vx;
                    m.y += m.vy;
                    m.life--;
                    const margin = 150;
                    if (m.life <= 0 || m.x < -margin || m.x > W + margin || m.y < -margin || m.y > H + margin) {
                        showerMeteors.splice(i, 1);
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                if (showerGenerating) {
                    const now = performance.now();
                    if (now - showerSpawnTimer > rand(cfg.spawnIntervalMin, cfg.spawnIntervalMax)) {
                        const cnt = randInt(1, 2);
                        for (let i = 0; i < cnt; i++) { if (showerMeteors.length < cfg.maxCount) spawnShowerMeteor(); }
                        showerSpawnTimer = now;
                    }
                }
            }

            function drawShowerMeteors() {
                for (const m of showerMeteors) drawShowerMeteor(m);
            }

            function drawShowerMeteor(m) {
                const { x, y, vx, vy, tailLength, life, maxLife, brightness, headColor, trailSegments } = m;
                const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy);
                if (len < 0.001) return;
                const dx = vx / len,
                    dy = vy / len;
                const lifeRatio = life / maxLife;
                const fadeOutStart = CONFIG.shower.fadeOutStart;
                let fadeAlpha = 1;
                if (lifeRatio < fadeOutStart) { fadeAlpha = lifeRatio / fadeOutStart; }
                const curTail = tailLength * (0.35 + 0.65 * lifeRatio);
                const segs = trailSegments;
                const baseW = rand(CONFIG.shower.baseWidthMin, CONFIG.shower.baseWidthMax);
                const tailCol = { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 };
                for (let i = 0; i < segs; i++) {
                    const t = i / segs,
                        col = lerpColor(headColor, tailCol, t);
                    const alpha = Math.pow(1 - t, 1.8) * brightness * 0.8 * fadeAlpha;
                    const width = baseW * (1 - t * 0.92);
                    const px = x - dx * t * curTail,
                        py = y - dy * t * curTail;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(px, py, Math.max(0.4, width), 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${col.r | 0},${col.g | 0},${col.b | 0},${alpha})`;
                    ctx.fill();
                    if (i < 4 && alpha > 0.1) {
                        ctx.shadowColor = `rgba(${col.r | 0},${col.g | 0},${col.b | 0},${alpha * 0.15})`;
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 15;
                        ctx.fill();
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    }
                }
                const headR = baseW * 3.0 + 2.0;
                const bright = Math.min(1.5, brightness * 0.9);
                const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, headR * 3.0);
                const hr = Math.min(255, headColor.r + 40),
                    hg = Math.min(255, headColor.g + 40),
                    hb = Math.min(255, headColor.b + 20);
                grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${hr},${hg},${hb},${bright * fadeAlpha})`);
                grad.addColorStop(0.2,
                    `rgba(${headColor.r},${headColor.g},${headColor.b},${bright * 0.8 * fadeAlpha})`);
                grad.addColorStop(0.6,
                    `rgba(${headColor.r * 0.6},${headColor.g * 0.5},${headColor.b * 0.3},${bright * 0.3 * fadeAlpha})`);
                grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,0)`);
                ctx.shadowColor = `rgba(${headColor.r},${headColor.g},${headColor.b},${bright * 0.4 * fadeAlpha})`;
                ctx.shadowBlur = 40;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(x, y, headR * 3.0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(x, y, headR * 0.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,245,230,${bright * 0.95 * fadeAlpha})`;
                ctx.shadowColor = `rgba(255,210,160,${bright * 0.3 * fadeAlpha})`;
                ctx.shadowBlur = 25;
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
            }

            function lerpColor(c1, c2, t) {
                return { r: c1.r + (c2.r - c1.r) * t, g: c1.g + (c2.g - c1.g) * t, b: c1.b + (c2.b - c1.b) * t };
            }

            // --- 视口系统 ---
            let viewport = {
                cx: W / 2,
                cy: H / 2,
                scale: 1.0,
                targetCx: W / 2,
                targetCy: H / 2,
                targetScale: 1.0,
                startCx: W / 2,
                startCy: H / 2,
                startScale: 1.0,
                progress: 1,
                duration: 60,
                transitioning: false,
                followMode: true,
                smooth: 0.08
            };

            function updateViewport() {
                if (viewport.transitioning) {
                    viewport.progress += 1 / viewport.duration;
                    if (viewport.progress >= 1) {
                        viewport.progress = 1;
                        viewport.transitioning = false;
                    }
                    const t = easeInOutQuad(viewport.progress);
                    viewport.cx = viewport.startCx + (viewport.targetCx - viewport.startCx) * t;
                    viewport.cy = viewport.startCy + (viewport.targetCy - viewport.startCy) * t;
                    viewport.scale = viewport.startScale + (viewport.targetScale - viewport.startScale) * t;
                } else if (viewport.followMode) {
                    if (meteor.state === 'flying') {
                        viewport.cx = viewport.targetCx;
                        viewport.cy = viewport.targetCy;
                        viewport.scale = viewport.targetScale;
                    } else if (meteor.state === 'broken') {
                        viewport.cx += (viewport.targetCx - viewport.cx) * viewport.smooth;
                        viewport.cy += (viewport.targetCy - viewport.cy) * viewport.smooth;
                        viewport.scale = viewport.targetScale;
                    } else {
                        viewport.cx = viewport.targetCx;
                        viewport.cy = viewport.targetCy;
                        viewport.scale = viewport.targetScale;
                    }
                }
            }

            function startTransition(toCx, toCy, toScale, dur) {
                viewport.startCx = viewport.cx;
                viewport.startCy = viewport.cy;
                viewport.startScale = viewport.scale;
                viewport.targetCx = toCx;
                viewport.targetCy = toCy;
                viewport.targetScale = toScale;
                viewport.progress = 0;
                viewport.duration = dur || 60;
                viewport.transitioning = true;
                viewport.followMode = true;
            }

            function setFollowTarget(cx, cy) {
                if (viewport.followMode && !viewport.transitioning) {
                    viewport.targetCx = cx;
                    viewport.targetCy = cy;
                }
            }

            // --- Epic 状态机 ---
            let epicFlowState = 'city_open';
            let cityOpen = { progress: 0, startTime: 0, duration: CONFIG.eye.duration };
            let cityRise = {
                progress: 0,
                startTime: 0,
                duration: CONFIG.rise.duration,
                maxOffset: CONFIG.rise.maxOffset
            };
            let cityFade = { progress: 0, startTime: 0, duration: CONFIG.fade.duration };
            let spaceOpen = { progress: 0, startTime: 0, duration: CONFIG.spaceOpen.duration };
            let cityOffsetY = 0;
            let eyeProgress = 0;
            let fadeStartOffset = 0;
            let epicEnded = false;
            let closingTriggered = false;
            let lastEpicState = '';

            function safeH() {
                if (isNaN(H) || H <= 0) { resize(); if (isNaN(H) || H <= 0) H = window.innerHeight || 600; }
                return H;
            }

            function initEpic() {
                generateCityStars();
                initSpaceStars();
                initClouds();
                initFloatParticles();
                generateBuildings();
                initPlanets();
                resetMeteor();
                epicFlowState = 'city_open';
                cityOpen.progress = 0;
                cityOpen.startTime = performance.now();
                cityRise.progress = 0;
                cityFade.progress = 0;
                spaceOpen.progress = 0;
                cityOffsetY = 0;
                fadeStartOffset = 0;
                eyeProgress = 0;
                showerMeteors = [];
                showerActive = false;
                showerGenerating = false;
                viewport.scale = 1.0;
                viewport.cx = W / 2;
                viewport.cy = H / 2;
                viewport.targetCx = W / 2;
                viewport.targetCy = H / 2;
                viewport.targetScale = 1.0;
                viewport.transitioning = false;
                viewport.followMode = true;
                epicEnded = false;
                closingTriggered = false;
                lastEpicState = '';
            }

            // --- Epic 绘制 ---
            function drawCityScene(offY, liftProgress, time) {
                drawCityStars(time, liftProgress);
                drawClouds(1.0);
                drawFloatParticles();
                for (const b of buildings) drawBuilding(b, offY);
                drawCityGlow(offY, CONFIG.glow.intensity, liftProgress);
                const vig = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
                vig.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                vig.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${CONFIG.visual.vignetteStrength})`);
                ctx.fillStyle = vig;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
            }

            function drawCrossFadeScene(offY, liftProgress, time, crossProgress) {
                drawCityStars(time, liftProgress, viewport, 1 - crossProgress);
                drawClouds(1.0);
                drawFloatParticles();
                for (const b of buildings) drawBuilding(b, offY);
                drawCityGlow(offY, CONFIG.glow.intensity, liftProgress);
                drawSpaceStars(time, viewport, crossProgress);
                const vig = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
                vig.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                vig.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${CONFIG.visual.vignetteStrength})`);
                ctx.fillStyle = vig;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
            }

            function drawSpaceScene(time) {
                drawSpaceStars(time, viewport);
                drawClouds(viewport.scale);
                drawFloatParticles();
                let planetScale = 1;
                let planetAlpha = 1;
                let meteorVisible = false;
                if (epicFlowState === 'space_open') {
                    planetScale = Math.max(0.02, smoothstep(0.02, 0.7, spaceOpen.progress));
                    planetAlpha = smoothstep(0.1, 0.8, spaceOpen.progress);
                    meteorVisible = false;
                } else if (epicFlowState === 'attached' || epicFlowState === 'flying' || epicFlowState === 'broken') {
                    planetScale = 1.0;
                    planetAlpha = 1.0;
                    meteorVisible = true;
                } else if (epicFlowState === 'retreat' || epicFlowState === 'meteor_shower' || epicFlowState ===
                    'ended') {
                    const normalizedScale = viewport.scale / 1.25;
                    planetScale = Math.max(0.02, normalizedScale);
                    planetAlpha = clamp((planetScale - 0.02) / (0.25 - 0.02), 0, 1);
                    meteorVisible = false;
                } else {
                    planetScale = 1.0;
                    planetAlpha = 1.0;
                    meteorVisible = false;
                }
                planetScale = Math.max(0.005, planetScale);
                ctx.save();
                ctx.translate(W / 2, H / 2);
                ctx.scale(viewport.scale, viewport.scale);
                ctx.translate(-viewport.cx, -viewport.cy);
                const dof = clamp((viewport.scale - 0.5) * 1.2, 0, 3);
                for (let i = 0; i < planets.length; i++) {
                    const p = planets[i],
                        blur = (i === 0) ? 0 : dof * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(i * 1.7));
                    const radiusScaled = p.radius * planetScale;
                    if (i === 0) { p.drawRings(ctx, p.x, p.y, radiusScaled, planetAlpha, 0); } else {
                        p.drawRings(ctx,
                            p.x, p.y, radiusScaled, planetAlpha * 0.7, blur);
                    }
                }
                for (let i = 0; i < planets.length; i++) {
                    const p = planets[i],
                        blur = (i === 0) ? 0 : dof * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(i * 1.7 + 0.5));
                    const radiusScaled = p.radius * planetScale;
                    if (i === 0) { p.draw(ctx, p.x, p.y, radiusScaled, planetAlpha, 0); } else {
                        p.draw(ctx, p.x, p.y,
                            radiusScaled, planetAlpha * 0.6, blur);
                    }
                }
                if (meteorVisible && (epicFlowState === 'flying' || epicFlowState === 'broken' || epicFlowState ===
                    'attached')) { drawMeteor(); }
                ctx.restore();
                if (epicFlowState === 'meteor_shower' || epicFlowState === 'ended') { drawShowerMeteors(); }
                const vstr = 0.08 + 0.12 * (1 - clamp((viewport.scale - 0.05) / 0.95, 0, 1));
                const vig = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
                vig.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                vig.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${vstr})`);
                ctx.fillStyle = vig;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                if (viewport.scale > CONFIG.visual.lensGlowStart) {
                    const ga = (viewport.scale - CONFIG.visual.lensGlowStart) / (1 - CONFIG.visual.lensGlowStart) *
                        CONFIG.visual.lensGlowAlpha;
                    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.min(W, H) * 0.3);
                    g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(200,180,255,${ga * 0.6})`);
                    g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(200,180,255,0)`);
                    ctx.fillStyle = g;
                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                }
            }

            function drawEyeMask(progress) {
                const p = Math.min(Math.max(progress, 0), 1);
                if (p >= 1) return;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                const cx = W / 2,
                    cy = H * 0.75;
                const maxR = Math.max(W, H) * 0.75,
                    ratio = 0.75;
                const rx = p * maxR,
                    ry = p * maxR * ratio;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.clip();
                const liftProg = (epicFlowState === 'city_rise') ? cityRise.progress : 0;
                drawCityScene(cityOffsetY, liftProg, performance.now() / 100);
                ctx.restore();
            }

            // --- Epic 更新 ---
            function updateEpic(timestamp) {
                const h = safeH();
                if (isNaN(h) || h <= 0) return;
                const time = timestamp / 100;

                if (epicFlowState !== lastEpicState) {
                    playEpicSoundByState(epicFlowState);
                    lastEpicState = epicFlowState;
                }

                switch (epicFlowState) {
                    case 'city_open': {
                        const elapsed = timestamp - cityOpen.startTime;
                        cityOpen.progress = Math.min(elapsed / cityOpen.duration, 1);
                        eyeProgress = easeOutCubic(cityOpen.progress);
                        const riseT = easeInOutQuad(cityOpen.progress);
                        const preOffset = riseT * CONFIG.rise.maxOffsetY * h * 0.4;
                        cityOffsetY = isNaN(preOffset) ? 0 : preOffset;
                        if (cityOpen.progress >= 1) {
                            epicFlowState = 'city_rise';
                            cityRise.startTime = timestamp;
                            cityRise.progress = 0;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'city_rise': {
                        const elapsed = timestamp - cityRise.startTime;
                        cityRise.progress = Math.min(elapsed / cityRise.duration, 1);
                        const t = easeInOutQuad(cityRise.progress);
                        const startOffset = CONFIG.rise.maxOffsetY * h * 0.4;
                        const targetOffset = CONFIG.rise.maxOffsetY * h;
                        cityOffsetY = startOffset + (targetOffset - startOffset) * t;
                        if (isNaN(cityOffsetY)) cityOffsetY = 0;
                        viewport.cy = H / 2 + cityOffsetY * CONFIG.fade.viewportFollow;
                        viewport.cx = W / 2;
                        viewport.scale = 1.0;
                        if (cityRise.progress >= 1) {
                            epicFlowState = 'city_fade';
                            cityFade.startTime = timestamp;
                            cityFade.progress = 0;
                            fadeStartOffset = cityOffsetY;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'city_fade': {
                        const elapsed = timestamp - cityFade.startTime;
                        cityFade.progress = Math.min(elapsed / cityFade.duration, 1);
                        const t = easeInOutQuad(cityFade.progress);
                        const dropAmount = t * h * CONFIG.fade.cityDrop;
                        cityOffsetY = fadeStartOffset + dropAmount;
                        if (isNaN(cityOffsetY)) cityOffsetY = fadeStartOffset;
                        viewport.scale = 1.0;
                        viewport.targetScale = 1.0;
                        viewport.cx = W / 2;
                        viewport.cy = H / 2 + cityOffsetY * CONFIG.fade.viewportFollow;
                        if (cityFade.progress >= 1) {
                            epicFlowState = 'space_open';
                            spaceOpen.startTime = timestamp;
                            spaceOpen.progress = 0;
                            const durFrames = Math.round(CONFIG.spaceOpen.duration / 16.67);
                            startTransition(attachInfo.x, attachInfo.y, CONFIG.spaceOpen.endScale, durFrames);
                            resetMeteor();
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'space_open': {
                        const elapsed = timestamp - spaceOpen.startTime;
                        spaceOpen.progress = Math.min(elapsed / spaceOpen.duration, 1);
                        if (!viewport.transitioning) {
                            epicFlowState = 'attached';
                            meteor.attachStartTime = performance.now();
                            viewport.targetCx = attachInfo.x;
                            viewport.targetCy = attachInfo.y;
                            viewport.targetScale = CONFIG.spaceOpen.endScale;
                            setTimeout(() => { infoEl.classList.add('show'); }, 500);
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'attached': {
                        if (!viewport.transitioning) {
                            viewport.targetCx = attachInfo.x;
                            viewport.targetCy = attachInfo.y;
                            viewport.targetScale = CONFIG.spaceOpen.endScale;
                        }
                        const elapsed = timestamp - meteor.attachStartTime;
                        if (elapsed >= CONFIG.meteor.attachDuration) triggerDetach();
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'flying': {
                        meteor.flyTimer++;
                        const prog = Math.min(meteor.flyTimer / 120, 1);
                        meteor.speed = CONFIG.meteor.startSpeed + (CONFIG.meteor.maxSpeed - CONFIG.meteor.startSpeed) *
                            prog * 0.6;
                        meteor.shadowBlur = CONFIG.meteor.startBlur + (CONFIG.meteor.maxBlur - CONFIG.meteor.startBlur) *
                            prog * 0.5;
                        meteor.vy += CONFIG.meteor.gravity;
                        const ang = Math.atan2(meteor.vy, meteor.vx);
                        const spd = meteor.speed;
                        meteor.vx = spd * Math.cos(ang);
                        meteor.vy = spd * Math.sin(ang);
                        meteor.x += meteor.vx;
                        meteor.y += meteor.vy;
                        meteor.history.push({ x: meteor.x, y: meteor.y });
                        if (meteor.history.length > CONFIG.meteor.historyLength) meteor.history.shift();
                        if (!viewport.transitioning) setFollowTarget(meteor.x, meteor.y);
                        if (meteor.flyTimer > meteor.flyDuration) breakMeteor();
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'broken': {
                        for (const p of meteor.brokenPieces) {
                            p.x += p.vx;
                            p.y += p.vy;
                            p.vy += 0.04;
                            p.life--;
                            p.alpha *= 0.993;
                            p.history.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y });
                            if (p.history.length > CONFIG.meteor.pieceHistoryLen) p.history.shift();
                        }
                        meteor.brokenPieces = meteor.brokenPieces.filter(p => p.life > 0 && p.alpha > 0.01);
                        if (!viewport.transitioning) {
                            if (meteor.brokenPieces.length > 0) {
                                let cx = 0,
                                    cy = 0;
                                for (const p of meteor.brokenPieces) {
                                    cx += p.x;
                                    cy += p.y;
                                }
                                cx /= meteor.brokenPieces.length;
                                cy /= meteor.brokenPieces.length;
                                setFollowTarget(cx, cy);
                            } else {
                                epicFlowState = 'retreat';
                                startTransition(viewport.cx, viewport.cy, 0.08, 120);
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'retreat': {
                        if (!viewport.transitioning && viewport.scale < 0.1) {
                            epicFlowState = 'meteor_shower';
                            showerActive = true;
                            showerGenerating = true;
                            showerStartTime = timestamp;
                            showerDuration = CONFIG.shower.duration;
                            showerSpawnTimer = timestamp;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'meteor_shower': {
                        if (showerActive) {
                            const elapsed = timestamp - showerStartTime;
                            if (!closingTriggered && elapsed > showerDuration * CONFIG_MASTER.showerTriggerRatio) {
                                closingTriggered = true;
                                showerGenerating = false;
                                startClosingPhase();
                            }
                            if (elapsed > showerDuration && !closingTriggered) {
                                closingTriggered = true;
                                showerGenerating = false;
                                startClosingPhase();
                            }
                        }
                        updateShowerMeteors();
                        if (!viewport.transitioning) {
                            viewport.targetScale = 0.08;
                            viewport.targetCx = attachInfo.x;
                            viewport.targetCy = attachInfo.y;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'ended': {
                        updateShowerMeteors();
                        if (!viewport.transitioning) {
                            viewport.targetScale = 0.08;
                            viewport.targetCx = attachInfo.x;
                            viewport.targetCy = attachInfo.y;
                        }
                        if (showerMeteors.length === 0 && !epicEnded) {
                            epicEnded = true;
                            infoEl.classList.remove('show');
                            if (!closingTriggered) {
                                closingTriggered = true;
                                startClosingPhase();
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
                for (const p of planets) p.update();
                updateFloatParticles(time);
                updateViewport();
            }

            function drawEpic(timestamp) {
                const time = timestamp / 100;
                const h = safeH();
                if (isNaN(h) || h <= 0) return;
                ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
                if (epicFlowState === 'city_open' || epicFlowState === 'city_rise') {
                    const offY = cityOffsetY || 0;
                    const liftProg = (epicFlowState === 'city_rise') ? cityRise.progress : 0;
                    drawCityScene(offY, liftProg, time);
                    if (epicFlowState === 'city_open') drawEyeMask(eyeProgress);
                } else if (epicFlowState === 'city_fade') {
                    const offY = cityOffsetY || 0;
                    const liftProg = 1;
                    let crossProgress = cityFade.progress;
                    crossProgress = easeInOutQuad(crossProgress);
                    drawCrossFadeScene(offY, liftProg, time, crossProgress);
                } else {
                    drawSpaceScene(time);
                }
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
                ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
                let status = '';
                if (epicFlowState === 'city_open') status = '✦ 华灯初上 ...';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'city_rise') status = '✦ 仰望星河 ...';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'city_fade') status = '✦ 飞向深空 ...';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'space_open') status = '✦ 行星显现 ...';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'attached') status = '✦ 附着于星环';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'flying') status = '✦ 陨星飞行中';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'broken') status = '✦ 陨星碎裂';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'retreat') status = '✦ 镜头拉远 ...';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'meteor_shower') status = '✦ 流星雨 ✦';
                else if (epicFlowState === 'ended') status = '✦ 星 空 ✦';
                ctx.fillText(status, 16, 26);
            }

            // ================================================================
            // 阶段二：闭眼 (CLOSING)
            // ================================================================
            let closingProgress = 0;
            let closeStartTime = 0;
            const CLOSE_DURATION = CONFIG_MASTER.firstCloseDuration;
            const CLOSE_DELAY = CONFIG_MASTER.firstHoldDuration;

            function startClosingPhase() {
                currentPhase = MASTER_PHASE.CLOSING;
                closeStartTime = performance.now();
                closingProgress = 0;
                infoEl.textContent = '✦ 晚安，好梦 ✦';
                infoEl.classList.remove('fireworks');
                infoEl.classList.add('show');
                showerGenerating = false;

                // 停止所有流星雨音效
                stopAllMeteorSounds();

                // 停止背景音
                stopCityAmbient();
                stopSpaceAmbient();

                if (soundInitialized && !soundMuted) {
                    playHeartbeat();
                }
            }

            function updateClosing(timestamp) {
                const elapsed = timestamp - closeStartTime;
                if (elapsed < CLOSE_DELAY) {
                    closingProgress = 0;
                    return;
                }
                const raw = (elapsed - CLOSE_DELAY) / CLOSE_DURATION;
                closingProgress = Math.min(raw, 1);
                if (closingProgress >= 1) {
                    startFireworksPhase();
                }
            }

            function drawClosing(timestamp) {
                drawEpic(timestamp);
                if (closingProgress > 0 && closingProgress < 1) {
                    const p = closingProgress;
                    const cx = W / 2,
                        cy = H / 2;
                    const maxR = Math.max(W, H) * 0.8;
                    const currentR = maxR * (1 - easeInOutSine(p));
                    const blur = 180;
                    const overlayGrad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, Math.max(0, currentR - blur), cx, cy, currentR +
                        blur);
                    overlayGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                    overlayGrad.addColorStop(0.6, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)');
                    overlayGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)');
                    ctx.fillStyle = overlayGrad;
                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                    if (currentR + blur < Math.max(W, H) / 2) {
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.rect(0, 0, W, H);
                        ctx.arc(cx, cy, currentR + blur, 0, Math.PI * 2, true);
                        ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                        ctx.fill();
                    }
                } else if (closingProgress >= 1) {
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                }
            }

            // ================================================================
            // 阶段三：烟花 (FIREWORKS)
            // ================================================================
            const FW_STAR_COLORS = [
                { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 245, b: 235 },
                { r: 235, g: 240, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 230, b: 210 },
                { r: 210, g: 220, b: 255 }, { r: 255, g: 210, b: 180 }
            ];
            let fwStars = [];

            function generateFwStars() {
                fwStars = [];
                const count = 260 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40);
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    const magnitude = Math.random();
                    const brightness = clamp(Math.pow(magnitude, 2.2), 0.05, 1.0);
                    const size = 0.3 + brightness * 2.5;
                    const isBright = brightness > 0.7 && Math.random() < 0.25;
                    const colorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * FW_STAR_COLORS.length);
                    const color = FW_STAR_COLORS[colorIdx];
                    const speed = 0.15 + Math.random() * 0.7;
                    const phase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const flickerAmt = 0.12 + brightness * 0.35;
                    const x = rand(10, W - 10);
                    const y = rand(10, H * 0.85);
                    fwStars.push({
                        x,
                        y,
                        size,
                        brightness,
                        baseAlpha: 0.5 + brightness * 0.5,
                        alpha: 0,
                        speed,
                        phase,
                        flickerAmt,
                        color,
                        isBright
                    });
                }
                fwStars.sort((a, b) => a.brightness - b.brightness);
            }
            generateFwStars();

            function updateFwStars(time) {
                for (const star of fwStars) {
                    const flicker = Math.sin(time * star.speed + star.phase);
                    const af = 0.5 + 0.5 * flicker;
                    const mod = 1 + star.flickerAmt * (af - 0.5) * 2;
                    star.alpha = star.baseAlpha * mod;
                    star.alpha = clamp(star.alpha, 0.04, 1);
                }
            }

            function drawFwStars(time, alphaMul) {
                const am = alphaMul || 1;
                for (const star of fwStars) {
                    const finalAlpha = star.alpha * am;
                    if (finalAlpha < 0.001) continue;
                    const { x, y, size, color, isBright } = star;
                    const { r, g, b } = color;
                    if (size > 0.6 && finalAlpha > 0.015) {
                        const glowR = size * (2.2 + star.brightness * 1.5);
                        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, glowR);
                        grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.25})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.07})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},0)`);
                        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(x, y, glowR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fill();
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha;
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(x, y, size * 0.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                    if (isBright && finalAlpha > 0.2 && size > 0.8) {
                        const len = 8 + size * 3;
                        ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha * 0.2;
                        ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.2})`;
                        ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
                        for (let ang = 0; ang < Math.PI * 2; ang += Math.PI / 4) {
                            const cosA = Math.cos(ang),
                                sinA = Math.sin(ang);
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.moveTo(x + cosA * 1.2, y + sinA * 1.2);
                            ctx.lineTo(x + cosA * len, y + sinA * len);
                            ctx.stroke();
                        }
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                }
            }

            let fwRockets = [];
            let fwParticles = [];
            let fwWind = 0;
            let fwWindTarget = 0;
            let fwTimerTick = 0;
            const FW_MAX_PARTICLES = 3200;
            const FW_AUTO_LAUNCH_RATE = 40;

            class FWParticle {
                constructor(x, y, vx, vy, opts = {}) {
                    this.x = x;
                    this.y = y;
                    this.vx = vx;
                    this.vy = vy;
                    this.gravity = opts.gravity !== undefined ? opts.gravity : 0.025;
                    this.drag = opts.drag !== undefined ? opts.drag : 0.93;
                    this.hue = opts.hue !== undefined ? opts.hue : rand(0, 360);
                    this.hueOffset = opts.hueOffset || rand(-18, 18);
                    this.sat = opts.sat || 100;
                    this.lit = opts.lit || rand(50, 72);
                    this.size = opts.size || rand(2.6, 4.6);
                    this.life = opts.life || rand(60, 100);
                    this.maxLife = this.life;
                    this.isGold = opts.isGold || false;
                    this.isWhite = opts.isWhite || false;
                    this.trail = [];
                    this.trailLength = opts.trailLength || 12;
                    this.twinkleSpeed = rand(0.08, 0.28);
                    this.twinklePhase = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);
                    this.windSensitivity = rand(0.4, 1.6);
                    this.alive = true;
                }
                update() {
                    this.life--;
                    if (this.life <= 0) { this.alive = false; return false; }
                    this.vx += (fwWind * this.windSensitivity) * 0.018;
                    this.vy += this.gravity;
                    this.vx *= this.drag;
                    this.vy *= this.drag;
                    this.x += this.vx;
                    this.y += this.vy;
                    this.trail.push({ x: this.x, y: this.y });
                    if (this.trail.length > this.trailLength) this.trail.shift();
                    return this.alive;
                }
                draw() {
                    if (!this.alive) return;
                    const progress = 1 - (this.life / this.maxLife);
                    let alpha = 1 - Math.pow(progress, 0.45);
                    if (alpha <= 0.001) return;
                    const twinkle = 0.75 + 0.25 * Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.004 * this.twinkleSpeed + this
                        .twinklePhase);
                    alpha *= twinkle;
                    let currentHue = this.hue;
                    let currentLit = this.lit;
                    if (this.isGold) {
                        currentHue = 45;
                        currentLit = 72;
                    }
                    if (this.isWhite) {
                        currentHue = 0;
                        currentLit = 100;
                    }
                    const hsl = (h, s, l, a) => `hsla(${h % 360}, ${s}%, ${l}%, ${a})`;
                    for (let i = 0; i < this.trail.length; i++) {
                        const t = i / this.trail.length;
                        const p = this.trail[i];
                        const trailAlpha = alpha * t * 0.45;
                        const trailSize = this.size * t * 0.75;
                        if (trailSize > 0.1) {
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, trailSize, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                            ctx.fillStyle = hsl(currentHue, this.sat, currentLit + 10, trailAlpha);
                            ctx.fill();
                        }
                    }
                    const glowSize = this.size * (2.6 + (1 - progress) * 2.2);
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(this.x, this.y, 0, this.x, this.y, glowSize);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255, 255, 255, ${alpha * 0.85})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.25, hsl(currentHue, this.sat, Math.min(92, currentLit + 32), alpha * 0.9));
                    grad.addColorStop(0.6, hsl(currentHue + this.hueOffset, this.sat, currentLit + 10, alpha * 0.5));
                    grad.addColorStop(1, hsl(currentHue + this.hueOffset * 1.5, this.sat, currentLit - 10, 0));
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, glowSize, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                }
            }

            class FWRocket {
                constructor(sx, sy, tx, ty) {
                    this.x = sx;
                    this.y = sy;
                    this.tx = tx;
                    this.ty = ty;
                    this.distTotal = Math.hypot(tx - sx, ty - sy);
                    this.distTraveled = 0;
                    this.angle = Math.atan2(ty - sy, tx - sx);
                    this.speed = rand(1.8, 2.8);
                    this.acc = 1.04;
                    this.hue = rand(0, 360);
                    this.type = randInt(0, 5);
                    this.alive = true;
                    this.trailCounter = 0;

                    const yNormLaunch = clamp(sy / H, 0, 1);
                    const baseLaunchVol = 0.08 + 0.90 * yNormLaunch;
                    const xCenterNormLaunch = Math.abs((sx / W) - 0.5) * 2;
                    const centerBoostLaunch = 1 - xCenterNormLaunch * 0.30;
                    const randLaunch = 0.75 + 0.50 * Math.random();
                    this.launchVolume = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.03, baseLaunchVol * centerBoostLaunch * randLaunch));

                    const yNormExplode = clamp(ty / H, 0, 1);
                    const baseExplodeVol = 0.10 + 0.88 * yNormExplode;
                    const xCenterNormExplode = Math.abs((tx / W) - 0.5) * 2;
                    const centerBoostExplode = 1 - xCenterNormExplode * 0.30;
                    const randExplode = 0.75 + 0.50 * Math.random();
                    this.explodeVolume = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.03, baseExplodeVol * centerBoostExplode * randExplode));

                    const launchPan = clamp(((sx / W) * 2 - 1), -1, 1);
                    playFireworkLaunchSound(launchPan, this.launchVolume);
                }

                update() {
                    this.speed *= this.acc;
                    const vx = Math.cos(this.angle) * this.speed;
                    const vy = Math.sin(this.angle) * this.speed;
                    this.x += vx;
                    this.y += vy;
                    this.distTraveled += this.speed;
                    this.trailCounter++;
                    if (this.trailCounter % 2 === 0 && Math.random() < 0.7) {
                        const p = new FWParticle(
                            this.x + rand(-2, 2),
                            this.y + rand(-2, 2),
                            rand(-0.6, 0.6),
                            rand(0.3, 1.8), {
                            hue: 35,
                            sat: 100,
                            lit: 60,
                            life: rand(12, 22),
                            size: rand(1.2, 2.2),
                            gravity: 0.02,
                            drag: 0.88,
                            trailLength: 3
                        }
                        );
                        if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
                    }
                    if (this.distTraveled >= this.distTotal * 0.92 || this.y < this.ty + 10) {
                        this.explode();
                        this.alive = false;
                        return false;
                    }
                    return true;
                }
                draw() {
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, 3.0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${this.hue}, 100%, 85%)`;
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                }
                explode() {
                    const x = this.x,
                        y = this.y,
                        hue = this.hue;
                    const pan = clamp(((x / W) * 2 - 1), -1, 1);
                    const yNorm = clamp(y / H, 0, 1);
                    const baseVol = 0.10 + 0.88 * yNorm;
                    const xCenterNorm = Math.abs((x / W) - 0.5) * 2;
                    const centerBoost = 1 - xCenterNorm * 0.30;
                    const randFactor = 0.75 + 0.50 * Math.random();
                    const finalVol = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.03, baseVol * centerBoost * randFactor));
                    playFireworkExplodeSound(pan, finalVol);

                    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
                        const angle = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);
                        const speed = rand(0.3, 2.4);
                        const p = new FWParticle(x, y,
                            Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                            Math.sin(angle) * speed, {
                            hue: hue + rand(-10, 10),
                            life: rand(40, 65),
                            size: rand(2.0, 3.6),
                            drag: 0.88,
                            hueOffset: 0,
                            trailLength: 5
                        }
                        );
                        if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
                    }
                    if (this.type === 0) {
                        for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
                            const angle = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);
                            const speed = rand(2.5, 10.0) * (Math.random() < 0.15 ? 1.6 : 1);
                            const p = new FWParticle(x, y,
                                Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                                Math.sin(angle) * speed, {
                                hue: hue + rand(-22, 22),
                                life: rand(70, 110),
                                size: rand(2.4, 5.2),
                                hueOffset: rand(12, 30),
                                trailLength: rand(8, 14)
                            }
                            );
                            if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
                        }
                    } else if (this.type === 1) {
                        const count = 100;
                        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                            const angle = (i / count) * Math.PI * 2;
                            if (Math.random() < 0.12) continue;
                            const radiusNoise = rand(0.75, 1.25);
                            const speed = 7.5 * radiusNoise;
                            const p = new FWParticle(x, y,
                                Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                                Math.sin(angle) * speed, {
                                hue: hue + rand(-8, 8),
                                life: rand(75, 110),
                                size: rand(2.6, 4.5),
                                drag: 0.95,
                                hueOffset: -8,
                                trailLength: rand(7, 12)
                            }
                            );
                            if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
                        }
                    } else if (this.type === 2) {
                        for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
                            const angle = rand(-Math.PI * 0.85, Math.PI * 0.85);
                            const speed = rand(4.0, 9.5);
                            const p = new FWParticle(x, y,
                                Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                                Math.sin(angle) * speed - 2.5, {
                                hue: hue + rand(-12, 12),
                                life: rand(100, 160),
                                size: rand(2.0, 3.6),
                                gravity: 0.07,
                                drag: 0.96,
                                trailLength: rand(12, 18),
                                windSensitivity: rand(1.2, 2.2),
                                isGold: Math.random() < 0.4
                            }
                            );
                            if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
