                        }
                    } else if (this.type === 3) {
                        const rays = randInt(18, 30);
                        for (let r = 0; r < rays; r++) {
                            const angle = (r / rays) * Math.PI * 2;
                            const perRay = randInt(5, 12);
                            for (let j = 0; j < perRay; j++) {
                                const speed = 2.0 + j * 1.5 + rand(-0.3, 0.3);
                                const p = new FWParticle(x, y,
                                    Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                                    Math.sin(angle) * speed, {
                                    hue: hue + j * 5 + rand(-5, 5),
                                    life: rand(80, 120),
                                    size: rand(2.6, 5.0),
                                    drag: 0.94,
                                    trailLength: rand(8, 14),
                                    hueOffset: rand(4, 16)
                                }
                                );
                                if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
                            }
                        }
                    } else if (this.type === 4) {
                        for (let i = 0; i < 150; i++) {
                            const angle = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);
                            const speed = rand(1.2, 11.0);
                            const p = new FWParticle(x, y,
                                Math.cos(angle) * speed,
                                Math.sin(angle) * speed, {
                                hue: hue + rand(-40, 40),
                                life: rand(55, 90),
                                size: rand(1.6, 4.2),
                                isWhite: Math.random() < 0.25,
                                twinkleSpeed: rand(0.15, 0.6),
                                trailLength: rand(5, 10),
                                hueOffset: rand(10, 30)
                            }
                            );
                            if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
                        }
                    } else {
                        for (let i = 0; i < 130; i++) {
                            const t = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);
                            const rBase = rand(3, 8);
                            const xOff = rBase * 1.2 * Math.sin(t);
                            const yOff = -rBase * 0.9 * Math.cos(t) - rBase * 0.4 * Math.sin(t) * Math.sin(t) + rBase *
                                0.5;
                            const speed = rand(1.8, 5.0);
                            const angle2 = Math.atan2(yOff, xOff) + rand(-0.3, 0.3);
                            const sp = speed * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.5);
                            const p = new FWParticle(x, y,
                                Math.cos(angle2) * sp,
                                Math.sin(angle2) * sp - 0.5, {
                                hue: hue + rand(-20, 20),
                                life: rand(70, 110),
                                size: rand(2.6, 4.5),
                                drag: 0.93,
                                trailLength: rand(8, 14),
                                gravity: 0.03
                            }
                            );
                            if (fwParticles.length < FW_MAX_PARTICLES) fwParticles.push(p);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            function launchFwFirework(targetX, targetY) {
                const sx = rand(W * 0.08, W * 0.92);
                const tx = targetX !== undefined ? targetX : rand(W * 0.08, W * 0.92);
                const ty = targetY !== undefined ? targetY : rand(H * 0.08, H * 0.42);
                const rocket = new FWRocket(sx, H + rand(5, 20), tx, ty);
                fwRockets.push(rocket);
            }

            function updateFwFireworks() {
                for (let i = fwRockets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                    const alive = fwRockets[i].update();
                    if (!alive) fwRockets.splice(i, 1);
                }
                for (let i = fwParticles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                    const alive = fwParticles[i].update();
                    if (!alive) fwParticles.splice(i, 1);
                }
                fwTimerTick++;
                if (fwTimerTick > FW_AUTO_LAUNCH_RATE) {
                    const count = Math.random() < 0.12 ? 2 : 1;
                    for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) { launchFwFirework(); }
                    fwTimerTick = 0;
                }
                fwWind += (fwWindTarget - fwWind) * 0.004;
                if (Math.random() < 0.004) fwWindTarget = rand(-1.8, 1.8);
            }

            function drawFwFireworks() {
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
                for (const rocket of fwRockets) { rocket.draw(); }
                for (const p of fwParticles) { p.draw(); }
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            }

            function drawFwOpenMask(progress, time) {
                const p = clamp(progress, 0, 1);
                if (p >= 1) {
                    drawFwScene(time);
                    return;
                }
                ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                const cx = W / 2,
                    cy = H * 0.68;
                const maxR = Math.max(W, H) * 0.75;
                const ratio = 0.72;
                const rx = p * maxR;
                const ry = p * maxR * ratio;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.clip();
                drawFwScene(time);
                if (p > 0.05 && p < 0.95) {
                    const glowAlpha = 0.035 * (1 - Math.abs(p - 0.5) * 2);
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, Math.max(W, H) * 0.6);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 240, 220, 0)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(255, 240, 220, 0)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.88, `rgba(180, 200, 255, ${glowAlpha * 0.6})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(180, 200, 255, 0)');
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(cx, cy, Math.max(W, H) * 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                }
                ctx.restore();
                const vig = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
                vig.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                vig.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${0.08 + (1 - p) * 0.12})`);
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                ctx.fillStyle = vig;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
            }

            function drawFwScene(time) {
                updateFwStars(time);
                ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                drawFwStars(time, 1);
                drawFwFireworks();
                const vig = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
                vig.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                vig.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.15)');
                ctx.fillStyle = vig;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
            }

            let fwStartTime = 0;
            let fwOpened = false;
            const FW_OPEN_DURATION = 2500;

            function startFireworksPhase() {
                currentPhase = MASTER_PHASE.FIREWORKS;
                fwStartTime = performance.now();
                fwOpened = false;
                fwRockets = [];
                fwParticles = [];
                fwWind = 0;
                fwWindTarget = 0;
                fwTimerTick = 0;
                fireworksStartTime = performance.now();
                fireworksPhaseReady = false;
                infoEl.textContent = '✦ 星夜 · 花火 ✦';
                infoEl.classList.add('fireworks');
                infoEl.classList.add('show');

                // 停止所有流星雨音效，确保烟花阶段干净
                stopAllMeteorSounds();

                // 停止城市和太空背景音
                stopCityAmbient();
                stopSpaceAmbient();

                for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                    setTimeout(() => launchFwFirework(), i * 120 + 80);
                }
                fireworksStartTime = performance.now();
                fireworksPhaseReady = false;
            }

            function updateFireworks(timestamp) {
                const elapsed = timestamp - fwStartTime;
                const raw = elapsed / FW_OPEN_DURATION;
                const openProgress = Math.min(raw, 1);
                if (openProgress >= 1) fwOpened = true;
                updateFwFireworks();
                if (fwOpened) {
                    const fwElapsed = timestamp - fireworksStartTime;
                    if (fwElapsed > CONFIG_MASTER.fireworksDuration && !fireworksPhaseReady) {
                        fireworksPhaseReady = true;
                        startCakeTransition();
                    }
                }
                return openProgress;
            }

            function drawFireworks(openProgress, time) {
                if (openProgress < 1) {
                    drawFwOpenMask(openProgress, time);
                } else {
                    drawFwScene(time);
                }
            }

            // ================================================================
            // 蛋糕过渡 + 蛋糕许愿 (自动播放生日歌)
            // ================================================================
            const OFF_W = 800;
            const OFF_H = 600;
            const offCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            offCanvas.width = OFF_W;
            offCanvas.height = OFF_H;
            const offCtx = offCanvas.getContext('2d');

            const DESK_Y = OFF_H * 0.78;
            const DESK_H = OFF_H - DESK_Y;
            const CAKE_CX = OFF_W / 2;
            const LAYER_H = 50;
            const LAYERS = [
                { w: 180, color: '#5c3a1a' },
                { w: 140, color: '#e8a0a0' },
                { w: 100, color: '#f5ede3' },
            ];
            const CANDLE_COLORS = ['#ff4444', '#ffdd44', '#4488ff', '#44dd44', '#ff66aa'];
            const CANDLE_W = 8;
            const CANDLE_H = 25;
            const CANDLE_COUNT = 5;
            const FLAME_W = 10;
            const FLAME_H = 14;

            let flamesExtinguished = false;

            function getCakeLayerY(index) { return DESK_Y - (index + 1) * LAYER_H; }

            function getCandlePositions() {
                const topW = LAYERS[2].w;
                const startX = CAKE_CX - topW / 2 + 8;
                const endX = CAKE_CX + topW / 2 - 8;
                const positions = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < CANDLE_COUNT; i++) {
                    const t = (i + 0.5) / CANDLE_COUNT;
                    positions.push(lerp(startX, endX, t));
                }
                return positions;
            }

            function drawCakeBackground() {
                const grad = offCtx.createRadialGradient(CAKE_CX, DESK_Y - 60, 30, CAKE_CX, DESK_Y - 60, 450);
                grad.addColorStop(0, '#2a1e18');
                grad.addColorStop(0.4, '#1e1612');
                grad.addColorStop(0.8, '#14100d');
                grad.addColorStop(1, '#0a0808');
                offCtx.fillStyle = grad;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, 0, OFF_W, OFF_H);
                const grad2 = offCtx.createRadialGradient(CAKE_CX, 0, 20, CAKE_CX, 0, 350);
                grad2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 180, 100, 0.04)');
                grad2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 180, 100, 0)');
                offCtx.fillStyle = grad2;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, 0, OFF_W, OFF_H);
                const envGlow = offCtx.createRadialGradient(CAKE_CX, DESK_Y - 40, 20, CAKE_CX, DESK_Y - 40, 400);
                envGlow.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 180, 100, 0.08)');
                envGlow.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(255, 140, 70, 0.04)');
                envGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 100, 50, 0)');
                offCtx.fillStyle = envGlow;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, 0, OFF_W, OFF_H);
            }

            function drawCakeDesk() {
                offCtx.save();
                const shadowGrad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(0, DESK_Y, 0, OFF_H);
                shadowGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                shadowGrad.addColorStop(0.15, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)');
                shadowGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)');
                offCtx.fillStyle = shadowGrad;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, DESK_Y, OFF_W, DESK_H);
                const deskGrad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(0, DESK_Y, 0, OFF_H);
                deskGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(70, 40, 25, 0.85)');
                deskGrad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(55, 32, 18, 0.90)');
                deskGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(40, 22, 12, 0.95)');
                offCtx.fillStyle = deskGrad;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, DESK_Y, OFF_W, DESK_H);
                offCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(90, 55, 35, 0.25)';
                offCtx.lineWidth = 0.5;
                for (let y = DESK_Y + 4; y < OFF_H; y += 6) {
                    const offset = Math.sin(y * 0.03) * 3 + Math.sin(y * 0.07 + 1) * 2;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.moveTo(0, y);
                    for (let x = 0; x < OFF_W; x += 2) {
                        const wave = Math.sin(x * 0.02 + y * 0.01) * 1.5 + Math.sin(x * 0.05 + y * 0.02) * 0.8;
                        offCtx.lineTo(x, y + wave + offset * 0.3);
                    }
                    offCtx.stroke();
                }
                offCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120, 80, 50, 0.12)';
                offCtx.lineWidth = 0.3;
                for (let y = DESK_Y + 2; y < OFF_H; y += 9) {
                    const offset = Math.sin(y * 0.04 + 2) * 4;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.moveTo(0, y);
                    for (let x = 0; x < OFF_W; x += 3) {
                        const wave = Math.sin(x * 0.015 + y * 0.008) * 2 + Math.sin(x * 0.04 + y * 0.015 + 1) * 1;
                        offCtx.lineTo(x, y + wave + offset * 0.2);
                    }
                    offCtx.stroke();
                }
                const highlight = offCtx.createLinearGradient(0, DESK_Y, 0, DESK_Y + 20);
                highlight.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(180, 130, 90, 0.10)');
                highlight.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(180, 130, 90, 0)');
                offCtx.fillStyle = highlight;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, DESK_Y, OFF_W, 20);
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeCandleGlow(time) {
                if (flamesExtinguished) return;
                offCtx.save();
                const glow1 = offCtx.createRadialGradient(CAKE_CX, DESK_Y - 50, 20, CAKE_CX, DESK_Y - 50, 350);
                glow1.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255, 200, 120, ${0.06 + 0.02 * Math.sin(time * 0.5 + 1)})`);
                glow1.addColorStop(0.3, `rgba(255, 170, 90, ${0.04 + 0.015 * Math.sin(time * 0.4 + 2)})`);
                glow1.addColorStop(0.7, `rgba(255, 140, 70, ${0.02 + 0.01 * Math.sin(time * 0.3 + 3)})`);
                glow1.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 100, 50, 0)');
                offCtx.fillStyle = glow1;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, 0, OFF_W, OFF_H);
                const candlePositions = getCandlePositions();
                for (let i = 0; i < candlePositions.length; i++) {
                    const cx = candlePositions[i];
                    const cy = DESK_Y - LAYER_H * 3 - CANDLE_H + 2;
                    const pulse = 0.85 + 0.15 * Math.sin(time * 1.2 + i * 1.8);
                    const radius = 60 * pulse;
                    const glow = offCtx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 5, cx, cy, radius);
                    const alpha = 0.12 + 0.06 * Math.sin(time * 0.7 + i * 1.2);
                    glow.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255, 210, 140, ${alpha})`);
                    glow.addColorStop(0.4, `rgba(255, 180, 100, ${alpha * 0.5})`);
                    glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 150, 80, 0)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = glow;
                    offCtx.fillRect(cx - radius, cy - radius, radius * 2, radius * 2);
                }
                const deskGlow = offCtx.createRadialGradient(CAKE_CX, DESK_Y + 20, 30, CAKE_CX, DESK_Y + 20, 240);
                deskGlow.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255, 190, 110, ${0.04 + 0.02 * Math.sin(time * 0.6 + 0.5)})`);
                deskGlow.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(255, 160, 80, ${0.02 + 0.01 * Math.sin(time * 0.5 + 1.5)})`);
                deskGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 130, 60, 0)');
                offCtx.fillStyle = deskGlow;
                offCtx.fillRect(0, DESK_Y, OFF_W, DESK_H);
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeCreamPiping(layerIndex) {
                const layer = LAYERS[layerIndex];
                const w = layer.w;
                const x = CAKE_CX - w / 2;
                const y = getCakeLayerY(layerIndex);
                let mainColor, dotColor;
                if (layerIndex === 0) {
                    mainColor = 'rgba(255, 180, 170, 0.9)';
                    dotColor = 'rgba(255, 210, 200, 0.7)';
                } else if (layerIndex === 1) {
                    mainColor = 'rgba(255, 220, 160, 0.9)';
                    dotColor = 'rgba(255, 235, 190, 0.7)';
                } else {
                    mainColor = 'rgba(190, 220, 255, 0.9)';
                    dotColor = 'rgba(210, 235, 255, 0.7)';
                }
                const radius = layerIndex === 0 ? 11 : (layerIndex === 1 ? 9 : 8);
                const spacing = layerIndex === 0 ? 22 : (layerIndex === 1 ? 20 : 18);
                const topY = y + 3;
                offCtx.save();
                const startX = x + radius + 5;
                const endX = x + w - radius - 5;
                for (let px = startX; px < endX; px += spacing) {
                    const offset = Math.sin(px * 0.5 + layerIndex * 2) * 1.5;
                    const cx = px + offset;
                    const cy = topY;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(cx, cy, radius, Math.PI, 0, false);
                    offCtx.strokeStyle = mainColor;
                    offCtx.lineWidth = 3.5;
                    offCtx.lineCap = 'round';
                    offCtx.stroke();
                    offCtx.fillStyle = dotColor;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(cx - 2, cy - 2, radius * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                }
                if (layerIndex === 2) {
                    for (let px = x + 18; px < x + w - 18; px += 24) {
                        const cx = px + Math.sin(px * 0.5) * 3;
                        const cy = topY + 8 + Math.sin(px * 0.4 + 1) * 5;
                        offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 200, 180, 0.8)';
                        offCtx.beginPath();
                        offCtx.arc(cx, cy, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        offCtx.fill();
                        offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4)';
                        offCtx.beginPath();
                        offCtx.arc(cx - 2, cy - 2, 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        offCtx.fill();
                    }
                } else if (layerIndex === 1) {
                    for (let px = x + 14; px < x + w - 14; px += 26) {
                        const cx = px + Math.sin(px * 0.6 + 2) * 2;
                        const cy = topY + 6 + Math.sin(px * 0.5 + 1.5) * 4;
                        offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 230, 170, 0.8)';
                        offCtx.beginPath();
                        offCtx.arc(cx, cy, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        offCtx.fill();
                    }
                } else {
                    for (let px = x + 12; px < x + w - 12; px += 28) {
                        const cx = px + Math.sin(px * 0.7 + 1) * 2;
                        const cy = topY + 5 + Math.sin(px * 0.6 + 2) * 3;
                        offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 200, 190, 0.7)';
                        offCtx.beginPath();
                        offCtx.arc(cx, cy, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        offCtx.fill();
                    }
                }
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeBody() {
                offCtx.save();
                for (let i = 1; i < LAYERS.length; i++) {
                    const layer = LAYERS[i];
                    const w = layer.w;
                    const x = CAKE_CX - w / 2;
                    const y = getCakeLayerY(i);
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 12;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 4;
                    offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0)';
                    offCtx.fillRect(x + 4, y + 2, w - 8, 6);
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 0;
                }
                for (let i = 0; i < LAYERS.length; i++) {
                    const layer = LAYERS[i];
                    const w = layer.w;
                    const x = CAKE_CX - w / 2;
                    const y = getCakeLayerY(i);
                    const h = LAYER_H;
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 15;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 3;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 5;
                    const grad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + w, y);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, darkenColor(layer.color, 30));
                    grad.addColorStop(0.2, lightenColor(layer.color, 10));
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, layer.color);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.8, darkenColor(layer.color, 15));
                    grad.addColorStop(1, darkenColor(layer.color, 40));
                    offCtx.fillStyle = grad;
                    roundRect(offCtx, x, y, w, h, 5);
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 0;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 0;
                    const hlGrad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(x, y + 3, x + w, y + 8);
                    hlGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
                    hlGrad.addColorStop(0.25, `rgba(255,255,255,${i === 2 ? 0.4 : 0.2})`);
                    hlGrad.addColorStop(0.75, `rgba(255,255,255,${i === 2 ? 0.3 : 0.12})`);
                    hlGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = hlGrad;
                    offCtx.fillRect(x + 8, y + 3, w - 16, 6);
                    const sideGrad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(x + w - 10, y, x + w, y + h);
                    sideGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.05)');
                    sideGrad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)');
                    sideGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = sideGrad;
                    offCtx.fillRect(x + w - 10, y, 10, h);
                    const leftGrad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + 5, y + h);
                    leftGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)');
                    leftGrad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)');
                    leftGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = leftGrad;
                    offCtx.fillRect(x, y, 5, h);
                    drawCakeCreamPiping(i);
                }
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeCandles() {
                const positions = getCandlePositions();
                const topY = getCakeLayerY(2);
                offCtx.save();
                for (let i = 0; i < positions.length; i++) {
                    const cx = positions[i];
                    const cy = topY;
                    const color = CANDLE_COLORS[i % CANDLE_COLORS.length];
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 6;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 1;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 2;
                    const grad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(cx - CANDLE_W / 2, cy, cx + CANDLE_W / 2, cy);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, darkenColor(color, 25));
                    grad.addColorStop(0.3, lightenColor(color, 20));
                    grad.addColorStop(0.7, color);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, darkenColor(color, 15));
                    offCtx.fillStyle = grad;
                    roundRect(offCtx, cx - CANDLE_W / 2, cy - CANDLE_H, CANDLE_W, CANDLE_H, 1.5);
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 0;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 0;
                    const hlGrad = offCtx.createLinearGradient(cx - CANDLE_W / 2, cy - CANDLE_H, cx - CANDLE_W / 2 + 2.5,
                        cy);
                    hlGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)');
                    hlGrad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)');
                    hlGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = hlGrad;
                    offCtx.fillRect(cx - CANDLE_W / 2 + 0.5, cy - CANDLE_H + 1, 2, CANDLE_H - 2);
                    offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 240, 220, 0.25)';
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.ellipse(cx, cy - CANDLE_H + 0.5, CANDLE_W / 2 - 0.5, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.fillStyle = '#333';
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(cx, cy - CANDLE_H + 1, 0.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                }
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeFlames(time) {
                if (flamesExtinguished) return;
                const positions = getCandlePositions();
                const topY = getCakeLayerY(2);
                offCtx.save();
                for (let i = 0; i < positions.length; i++) {
                    const cx = positions[i];
                    const baseY = topY - CANDLE_H + 1;
                    const phase = i * 1.3 + 0.7;
                    const scaleX = 0.92 + 0.08 * Math.sin(time * 1.2 + phase);
                    const scaleY = 0.90 + 0.10 * Math.sin(time * 1.6 + phase * 0.7);
                    const offsetY = 1.2 * Math.sin(time * 2.0 + phase * 1.2);
                    const flameX = cx;
                    const flameY = baseY + offsetY - 2;
                    const glowRadius = 18 * (0.92 + 0.08 * Math.sin(time * 1.0 + phase));
                    const glow = offCtx.createRadialGradient(flameX, flameY, 2, flameX, flameY, glowRadius);
                    const glowA = 0.12 + 0.05 * Math.sin(time * 0.7 + phase);
                    glow.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255, 200, 100, ${glowA})`);
                    glow.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(255, 160, 70, ${glowA * 0.5})`);
                    glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 120, 50, 0)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = glow;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(flameX, flameY, glowRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.save();
                    offCtx.translate(flameX, flameY);
                    offCtx.scale(scaleX, scaleY);
                    const grad = offCtx.createRadialGradient(0, -3, 1, 0, 0, FLAME_H * 0.6);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 251, 230, 1)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.15, 'rgba(255, 221, 68, 1)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.4, 'rgba(255, 170, 0, 1)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(255, 119, 0, 1)');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 68, 0, 0.5)');
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    const w = FLAME_W / 2,
                        h = FLAME_H / 2;
                    offCtx.moveTo(0, -h);
                    offCtx.bezierCurveTo(w * 0.8, -h * 0.4, w, -h * 0.1, w, h * 0.2);
                    offCtx.bezierCurveTo(w, h * 0.6, w * 0.6, h, 0, h * 0.85);
                    offCtx.bezierCurveTo(-w * 0.6, h, -w, h * 0.6, -w, h * 0.2);
                    offCtx.bezierCurveTo(-w, -h * 0.1, -w * 0.8, -h * 0.4, 0, -h);
                    offCtx.closePath();
                    offCtx.fillStyle = grad;
                    offCtx.fill();
                    const coreGrad = offCtx.createRadialGradient(0, -2, 0.5, 0, 0, FLAME_H * 0.25);
                    coreGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 255, 240, 0.9)');
                    coreGrad.addColorStop(0.3, 'rgba(255, 230, 150, 0.6)');
                    coreGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 200, 80, 0)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = coreGrad;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.ellipse(0, -2, FLAME_W * 0.25, FLAME_H * 0.25, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.restore();
                    const baseGlow = offCtx.createRadialGradient(cx, baseY + 1, 1, cx, baseY + 1, 12);
                    const bA = 0.15 + 0.08 * Math.sin(time * 0.8 + phase);
                    baseGlow.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255, 200, 100, ${bA})`);
                    baseGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255, 150, 70, 0)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = baseGlow;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(cx, baseY + 1, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                }
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeGift(x, y, w, h, boxColor, ribbonColor, shadowOffset) {
                shadowOffset = shadowOffset || 5;
                if (!isFinite(x) || !isFinite(y) || !isFinite(w) || !isFinite(h) || w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
                offCtx.save();
                offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
                offCtx.shadowBlur = 10;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 3;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetY = shadowOffset;
                const gradBox = offCtx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + w, y + h);
                gradBox.addColorStop(0, lightenColor(boxColor, 25));
                gradBox.addColorStop(0.5, boxColor);
                gradBox.addColorStop(1, darkenColor(boxColor, 30));
                offCtx.fillStyle = gradBox;
                roundRect(offCtx, x, y, w, h, 4);
                offCtx.fill();
                offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 0;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 0;
                offCtx.fillStyle = ribbonColor;
                offCtx.fillRect(x + w / 2 - 3, y, 6, h);
                offCtx.fillRect(x, y + h / 2 - 3, w, 6);
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.arc(x + w / 2 - 7, y - 3, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                offCtx.fill();
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.arc(x + w / 2 + 7, y - 3, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                offCtx.fill();
                offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)';
                offCtx.fillRect(x + 6, y + 3, w - 12, 4);
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            const giftPyramidData = (function () {
                const layerDefs = [
                    { layer: 5, yBase: -160, count: 1, xStart: 140, xEnd: 170, sizeMin: 30, sizeMax: 40 },
                    { layer: 4, yBase: -128, count: 2, xStart: 120, xEnd: 190, sizeMin: 34, sizeMax: 44 },
                    { layer: 3, yBase: -96, count: 3, xStart: 100, xEnd: 210, sizeMin: 38, sizeMax: 48 },
                    { layer: 2, yBase: -64, count: 4, xStart: 80, xEnd: 225, sizeMin: 42, sizeMax: 54 },
                    { layer: 1, yBase: -32, count: 5, xStart: 60, xEnd: 240, sizeMin: 46, sizeMax: 58 },
                    { layer: 0, yBase: 0, count: 6, xStart: 40, xEnd: 260, sizeMin: 50, sizeMax: 64 },
                ];
                const allGifts = [];
                const colorPalette = [
                    ['#e74c3c', '#f1c40f'],
                    ['#2980b9', '#ecf0f1'],
                    ['#27ae60', '#f1c40f'],
                    ['#f39c12', '#e74c3c'],
                    ['#8e44ad', '#f1c40f'],
                    ['#1abc9c', '#e67e22'],
                    ['#e67e22', '#2c3e50'],
                    ['#2c3e50', '#f1c40f'],
                    ['#c0392b', '#ecf0f1'],
                    ['#16a085', '#f39c12'],
                    ['#d35400', '#f1c40f'],
                    ['#9b59b6', '#ecf0f1'],
                ];
                let idx = 0;
                for (let ld of layerDefs) {
                    const { layer, yBase, count, xStart, xEnd, sizeMin, sizeMax } = ld;
                    const availableWidth = xEnd - xStart;
                    let rawWidths = [];
                    let totalRawWidth = 0;
                    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                        let w = sizeMin + Math.random() * (sizeMax - sizeMin);
                        rawWidths.push(w);
                        totalRawWidth += w;
                    }
                    let scale = availableWidth / totalRawWidth;
                    let currentX = xStart;
                    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                        const w = rawWidths[i] * scale;
                        const h = w * (0.78 + Math.random() * 0.12);
                        const x = currentX;
                        const y = DESK_Y + yBase - h;
                        const ci = idx % colorPalette.length;
                        const [boxColor, ribbonColor] = colorPalette[ci];
                        const darkAmt = (5 - layer) * 2;
                        const finalColor = darkenColor(boxColor, darkAmt);
                        const shadowOff = Math.max(3, 8 - layer);
                        allGifts.push({ x, y, w, h, color: finalColor, ribbon: ribbonColor, layer, shadowOff });
                        currentX += w;
                        idx++;
                    }
                }
                allGifts.sort((a, b) => b.layer - a.layer);
                return allGifts;
            })();

            function drawCakeGiftPyramid() {
                for (let g of giftPyramidData) {
                    drawCakeGift(g.x, g.y, g.w, g.h, g.color, g.ribbon, g.shadowOff);
                }
            }

            const cakeBalloons = [];
            const balloonColors = ['#ff6b81', '#ffd93d', '#6bcfff', '#a29bfe', '#ff9ff3', '#ff9f43', '#00d2d3', '#f368e0',
                '#ff6b81', '#ffd93d'
            ];
            const numBalloons = 12;
            for (let i = 0; i < numBalloons; i++) {
                cakeBalloons.push({
                    x: 570 + Math.random() * 210,
                    y: 210 + Math.random() * 160,
                    color: balloonColors[i % balloonColors.length],
                    phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
                    speed: 0.4 + Math.random() * 0.3,
                    swingSpeed: 0.015 + Math.random() * 0.015,
                    radius: 14 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8)
                });
            }

            function drawCakeBalloons(time) {
                for (let b of cakeBalloons) {
                    const baseX = b.x;
                    const baseY = DESK_Y - 2;
                    const grad = offCtx.createRadialGradient(baseX, baseY, 1, baseX, baseY, 10);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, b.color);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, lightenColor(b.color, 50));
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
                    offCtx.fillStyle = grad;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(baseX, baseY, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.fillStyle = b.color;
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 6;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(baseX, baseY, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(baseX - 1.5, baseY - 1.5, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                }
                for (let b of cakeBalloons) {
                    const floatOffset = Math.sin(time * b.speed + b.phase) * 5;
                    const swingOffset = Math.sin(time * b.swingSpeed * 2 + b.phase) * 4;
                    const currentX = b.x + swingOffset;
                    const currentY = b.y + floatOffset;
                    const baseX = b.x;
                    const baseY = DESK_Y - 2;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.moveTo(currentX, currentY + b.radius * 0.9);
                    offCtx.lineTo(baseX, baseY);
                    offCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(200,200,200,0.3)';
                    offCtx.lineWidth = 1.2;
                    offCtx.stroke();
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 8;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 2;
                    offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 3;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.ellipse(currentX, currentY, b.radius, b.radius * 1.3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fillStyle = b.color;
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                    offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.ellipse(currentX - b.radius * 0.25, currentY - b.radius * 0.3, b.radius * 0.3, b.radius * 0.4,
                        -0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)';
                    offCtx.fill();
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.moveTo(currentX, currentY + b.radius * 0.9);
                    offCtx.lineTo(currentX - 2, currentY + b.radius * 1.1);
                    offCtx.lineTo(currentX + 2, currentY + b.radius * 1.1);
                    offCtx.fillStyle = darkenColor(b.color, 30);
                    offCtx.fill();
                }
            }

            function drawCakeBanner() {
                const bannerW = OFF_W - 20;
                const bannerH = 55;
                const bx = (OFF_W - bannerW) / 2;
                const by = 80;
                const curveDepth = 22;
                offCtx.save();
                const gradient = offCtx.createLinearGradient(bx, 0, bx, by + bannerH + curveDepth);
                gradient.addColorStop(0, '#e74c3c');
                gradient.addColorStop(0.5, '#c0392b');
                gradient.addColorStop(1, '#a93226');
                offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
                offCtx.shadowBlur = 14;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 2;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 4;
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.moveTo(bx, by);
                offCtx.quadraticCurveTo(CAKE_CX, by + curveDepth, bx + bannerW, by);
                offCtx.lineTo(bx + bannerW, by + bannerH + curveDepth);
                offCtx.quadraticCurveTo(CAKE_CX, by + bannerH + curveDepth * 2, bx, by + bannerH + curveDepth);
                offCtx.closePath();
                offCtx.fillStyle = gradient;
                offCtx.fill();
                offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                offCtx.strokeStyle = '#f1c40f';
                offCtx.lineWidth = 3;
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.moveTo(bx, by);
                offCtx.quadraticCurveTo(CAKE_CX, by + curveDepth, bx + bannerW, by);
                offCtx.lineTo(bx + bannerW, by + bannerH + curveDepth);
                offCtx.quadraticCurveTo(CAKE_CX, by + bannerH + curveDepth * 2, bx, by + bannerH + curveDepth);
                offCtx.closePath();
                offCtx.stroke();
                const triSize = 20;
                offCtx.fillStyle = '#f1c40f';
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.moveTo(bx, by);
                offCtx.lineTo(bx - triSize, by + curveDepth / 2 + bannerH / 2);
                offCtx.lineTo(bx, by + bannerH + curveDepth);
                offCtx.closePath();
                offCtx.fill();
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.moveTo(bx + bannerW, by);
                offCtx.lineTo(bx + bannerW + triSize, by + curveDepth / 2 + bannerH / 2);
                offCtx.lineTo(bx + bannerW, by + bannerH + curveDepth);
                offCtx.closePath();
                offCtx.fill();
                const cakeX = CAKE_CX - 150;
                const cakeY = by + bannerH / 2 + curveDepth * 0.8;
                offCtx.fillStyle = '#f1c40f';
                offCtx.fillRect(cakeX - 12, cakeY, 24, 14);
                offCtx.fillStyle = '#fff';
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.arc(cakeX, cakeY, 12, Math.PI, 0);
                offCtx.fill();
                offCtx.fillStyle = '#e74c3c';
                offCtx.beginPath();
                offCtx.arc(cakeX, cakeY - 3, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                offCtx.fill();
                const giftX = CAKE_CX + 150;
                const giftY = by + bannerH / 2 + curveDepth * 0.8;
                offCtx.fillStyle = '#e67e22';
                offCtx.fillRect(giftX - 10, giftY - 14, 20, 16);
                offCtx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
                offCtx.lineWidth = 2;
                offCtx.strokeRect(giftX - 10, giftY - 14, 20, 16);
                offCtx.fillStyle = '#e74c3c';
                offCtx.fillRect(giftX - 4, giftY - 14, 8, 16);
                offCtx.textAlign = 'center';
                offCtx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                offCtx.font = 'bold 40px "Arial", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif';
                offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
                offCtx.shadowBlur = 4;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetX = 1;
                offCtx.shadowOffsetY = 2;
                const text = '生日快乐';
                const chars = text.split('');
                const charWidth = 50;
                const startX = CAKE_CX - (chars.length - 1) * charWidth / 2;
                for (let i = 0; i < chars.length; i++) {
                    const x = startX + i * charWidth;
                    const normalizedX = (2 * (x - CAKE_CX)) / bannerW;
                    const droop = curveDepth * (1 - normalizedX * normalizedX);
                    const y = by + bannerH / 2 + droop;
                    offCtx.fillStyle = '#f1c40f';
                    offCtx.fillText(chars[i], x, y + 1);
                    offCtx.fillStyle = '#fff8e7';
                    offCtx.fillText(chars[i], x, y);
                }
                offCtx.font = 'bold 14px "Arial", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif';
                const enText = 'HAPPY BIRTHDAY';
                offCtx.fillStyle = '#f1c40f';
                const enY = by + bannerH + curveDepth + 3;
                offCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
                offCtx.shadowBlur = 2;
                offCtx.fillText(enText, CAKE_CX, enY);
                offCtx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
                offCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
                offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.8)';
                for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
                    const angle = i / 10 * Math.PI * 2 + 0.3;
                    const dist = 130 + Math.sin(i * 1.3) * 15;
                    const sx = CAKE_CX + Math.cos(angle) * dist;
                    const sy = by + bannerH / 2 + Math.sin(angle) * dist * 0.35;
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    for (let j = 0; j < 5; j++) {
                        const a = (j * 4 * Math.PI / 5) - Math.PI / 2;
                        const px = sx + 4 * Math.cos(a);
                        const py = sy + 4 * Math.sin(a);
                        if (j === 0) offCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                        else offCtx.lineTo(px, py);
                    }
                    offCtx.closePath();
                    offCtx.fill();
                }
                offCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';
                for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
                    const sx = bx + 40 + i * (bannerW / 8);
                    const normalizedX = (2 * (sx - CAKE_CX)) / bannerW;
                    const droop = curveDepth * (1 - normalizedX * normalizedX);
                    offCtx.beginPath();
                    offCtx.arc(sx, by + 12 + droop * 0.6, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    offCtx.fill();
                }
                offCtx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeScene(time) {
                offCtx.clearRect(0, 0, OFF_W, OFF_H);
                drawCakeBackground();
                drawCakeDesk();
                drawCakeBody();
                drawCakeCandleGlow(time);
                drawCakeGiftPyramid();
                drawCakeBalloons(time);
                drawCakeBanner();
                drawCakeCandles();
                drawCakeFlames(time);
            }

            let cakeDrawInfo = { offsetX: 0, offsetY: 0, drawW: 0, drawH: 0, scale: 1 };

            function updateCakeDrawInfo() {
                const scaleX = W / OFF_W;
                const scaleY = H / OFF_H;
                const scale = Math.min(scaleX, scaleY);
                const drawW = OFF_W * scale;
                const drawH = OFF_H * scale;
                const offsetX = (W - drawW) / 2;
                const offsetYPos = (H - drawH) / 2;
                cakeDrawInfo = { offsetX, offsetY: offsetYPos, drawW, drawH, scale };
            }

            function drawCakeToMain() {
                updateCakeDrawInfo();
                const { offsetX, offsetY, drawW, drawH } = cakeDrawInfo;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.drawImage(offCanvas, offsetX, offsetY, drawW, drawH);
                ctx.restore();
            }

            function drawCakeWithOpenMask(progress) {
                const p = clamp(progress, 0, 1);
                const cx = W / 2;
                const cy = H * 0.72;
                const maxR = Math.max(W, H) * 0.75;
                const ratio = 0.75;
                const rx = p * maxR;
                const ry = p * maxR * ratio;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0808';
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.clip();
                drawCakeToMain();
                ctx.restore();
                if (p > 0.05 && p < 0.95) {
                    const glowAlpha = 0.035 * (1 - Math.abs(p - 0.5) * 2);
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, Math.max(W, H) * 0.6);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 240, 220, 0)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(255, 240, 220, 0)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.88, `rgba(180, 200, 255, ${glowAlpha * 0.6})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(180, 200, 255, 0)');
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(cx, cy, Math.max(W, H) * 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                }
            }

            function drawCakeEyeCloseMask(progress) {
                const p = clamp(progress, 0, 1);
                if (p >= 0.99) {
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                    return;
                }
                if (p <= 0) return;
                const { offsetX, offsetY, drawW, drawH } = cakeDrawInfo;
                const cx = offsetX + drawW / 2;
                const cy = offsetY + drawH / 2;
                const maxR = Math.max(drawW, drawH) * 0.65;
                const currentR = maxR * (1 - easeInOutSine(p));
                const blur = Math.max(10, maxR * 0.25 * (1 - p * 0.8));
                if (currentR < 5) {
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                    return;
                }
                const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(
                    cx, cy, Math.max(0, currentR - blur),
                    cx, cy, currentR + blur
                );
                grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                grad.addColorStop(0.6, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)');
                grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)');
                ctx.save();
                ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                ctx.restore();
                if (p > 0.2 && p < 0.85) {
                    const intensity = Math.sin(p * Math.PI) * 0.08;
                    ctx.save();
                    for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
                        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                        const dist = 50 + Math.random() * 300;
                        const sx = cx + Math.cos(angle) * dist;
                        const sy = cy + Math.sin(angle) * dist * 0.7;
                        const size = 0.6 + Math.random() * 1.6;
                        const alpha = (0.2 + Math.random() * 0.6) * intensity;
                        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
                        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(sx, sy, size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fill();
                    }
                    ctx.restore();
                }
            }

            // --- 烟雾粒子 ---
            let cakeSmokeParticles = [];
            let cakeSmokeActive = false;

            function createCakeSmoke() {
                updateCakeDrawInfo();
                const positions = getCandlePositions();
                const topY = getCakeLayerY(2);
                const { offsetX, offsetY, scale } = cakeDrawInfo;
                cakeSmokeParticles = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < positions.length; i++) {
                    const cx = offsetX + positions[i] * scale;
                    const cy = offsetY + (topY - CANDLE_H + 2) * scale;
                    const count = 14 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
                    for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
                        const angle = rand(0, Math.PI * 2);
                        const dist = rand(0, 18 * scale);
                        const gray = rand(40, 100);
                        cakeSmokeParticles.push({
                            x: cx + Math.cos(angle) * dist,
                            y: cy + Math.sin(angle) * dist - rand(0, 10 * scale),
                            vx: rand(-0.8 * scale, 0.8 * scale),
                            vy: rand(-2.5 * scale, -4.5 * scale),
                            radius: rand(12 * scale, 30 * scale),
                            alpha: rand(0.4, 0.7),
                            life: 1.0,
                            decay: 0.0005 + rand(0.0002, 0.0012),
                            grow: rand(0.02 * scale, 0.06 * scale),
                            r: gray + rand(-15, 15),
                            g: gray + rand(-15, 15),
                            b: gray + rand(-10, 20),
                        });
                    }
                }
                cakeSmokeActive = true;
            }

            function updateCakeSmoke() {
                if (!cakeSmokeActive) return;
                cakeSmokeParticles.forEach(p => {
                    p.x += p.vx + rand(-0.05, 0.05);
                    p.y += p.vy;
                    p.vy -= 0.012;
                    p.vx += rand(-0.015, 0.015);
                    p.radius += p.grow;
                    p.alpha *= (1 - p.decay);
                    p.life -= p.decay;
                });
                cakeSmokeParticles = cakeSmokeParticles.filter(p => p.alpha > 0.01);
                if (cakeSmokeParticles.length === 0) {
                    cakeSmokeActive = false;
                }
            }

            function drawCakeSmoke() {
                if (!cakeSmokeActive) return;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                cakeSmokeParticles.forEach(p => {
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x, p.y, 0, p.x, p.y, p.radius);
                    const a = p.alpha * 0.3;
                    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${p.r}, ${p.g}, ${p.b}, ${a * 0.6})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(${p.r - 10}, ${p.g - 10}, ${p.b - 5}, ${a * 0.35})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${p.r - 25}, ${p.g - 25}, ${p.b - 15}, 0)`);
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                });
                ctx.restore();
            }

            // --- 星空场景 (用于蛋糕祝福) ---
            let stars_stars = [];
            let stars_clouds = [];
            const STAR_COLORS = [
                { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 },
                { r: 255, g: 245, b: 235 },
                { r: 235, g: 240, b: 255 },
                { r: 255, g: 230, b: 210 },
                { r: 210, g: 220, b: 255 },
                { r: 255, g: 210, b: 180 },
            ];

            function stars_generateStars(w, h) {
                const list = [];
                const count = 280 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 60);
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    let magnitude = Math.random();
                    let brightness = Math.pow(magnitude, 2.5);
                    brightness = clamp(brightness, 0.05, 1.0);
                    const size = 0.3 + brightness * 2.7;
                    const isBright = brightness > 0.7 && Math.random() < 0.3;
                    let colorIdx;
                    if (brightness > 0.7) colorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
                    else if (brightness > 0.4) colorIdx = 3 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
                    else colorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * STAR_COLORS.length);
                    const color = STAR_COLORS[colorIdx % STAR_COLORS.length];
                    const speed = 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.8;
                    const phase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const flickerAmt = 0.15 + brightness * 0.35;
                    const x = Math.random() * (w - 20) + 10;
                    const y = Math.random() * (h * 0.75) + 10;
                    const randomDelay = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08;
                    list.push({
                        x,
                        y,
                        size,
                        brightness,
                        baseAlpha: 0.6 + brightness * 0.4,
                        alpha: 0,
                        speed,
                        phase,
                        flickerAmt,
                        color,
                        isBright,
                        randomDelay,
                    });
                }
                return list;
            }

            function stars_updateStars(time) {
                for (const star of stars_stars) {
                    const flicker = Math.sin(time * star.speed + star.phase);
                    const af = 0.5 + 0.5 * flicker;
                    const mod = 1 + star.flickerAmt * (af - 0.5) * 2;
                    star.alpha = star.baseAlpha * mod;
                    star.alpha = clamp(star.alpha, 0.05, 1);
                }
            }

            function stars_drawStarsLayer(time) {
                stars_updateStars(time);
                const sorted = [...stars_stars].sort((a, b) => b.brightness - a.brightness);
                for (const star of sorted) {
                    const yNorm = star.y / (H * 0.75);
                    const heightFactor = 1 - yNorm;
                    const atmosphere = 0.5 + 0.5 * heightFactor;
                    const sizeFactor = 0.6 + 0.4 * heightFactor;
                    const finalAlpha = star.alpha * atmosphere;
                    if (finalAlpha < 0.001) continue;
                    const finalSize = star.size * sizeFactor;
                    const { x, y, color, isBright } = star;
                    const { r, g, b } = color;
                    if (finalSize > 0.8 && finalAlpha > 0.02) {
                        const glowR = finalSize * 2.5;
                        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, glowR);
                        grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.35})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.10})`);
                        grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${r},${g},${b},0)`);
                        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.arc(x, y, glowR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                        ctx.fill();
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha;
                    ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(x, y, finalSize * 0.85, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                    if (isBright && finalAlpha > 0.2) {
                        const len = 10 + finalSize * 4;
                        for (let ang = 0; ang < Math.PI * 2; ang += Math.PI / 4) {
                            const cosA = Math.cos(ang),
                                sinA = Math.sin(ang);
                            ctx.globalAlpha = finalAlpha * 0.25;
                            ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${finalAlpha * 0.25})`;
                            ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
                            ctx.beginPath();
                            ctx.moveTo(x + cosA * 1.5, y + sinA * 1.5);
                            ctx.lineTo(x + cosA * len, y + sinA * len);
                            ctx.stroke();
                        }
                    }
                    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                }
            }

            function stars_generateClouds(w, h) {
                const list = [];
                const count = 25 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 25);
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    const cx = Math.random() * w;
                    const cy = Math.random() * h * 0.6;
                    const radius = 60 + Math.random() * 250;
                    const alpha = 0.008 + Math.random() * 0.03;
                    const colorShift = Math.random();
                    let r, g, b;
                    if (colorShift < 0.33) {
                        r = 80;
                        g = 120;
                        b = 200;
                    } else if (colorShift < 0.66) {
                        r = 120;
                        g = 100;
                        b = 180;
                    } else {
                        r = 200;
                        g = 180;
                        b = 160;
                    }
                    list.push({ x: cx, y: cy, radius, alpha, r, g, b });
                }
                return list;
            }

            function stars_drawClouds() {
                if (stars_clouds.length === 0) return;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                for (const cloud of stars_clouds) {
                    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cloud.x, cloud.y, 0, cloud.x, cloud.y, cloud.radius);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${cloud.r}, ${cloud.g}, ${cloud.b}, ${cloud.alpha})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(${cloud.r}, ${cloud.g}, ${cloud.b}, ${cloud.alpha * 0.5})`);
                    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${cloud.r}, ${cloud.g}, ${cloud.b}, 0)`);
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(cloud.x, cloud.y, cloud.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();
                }
                ctx.restore();
            }

            function stars_drawFullScene(time) {
                ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                stars_drawStarsLayer(time);
                stars_drawClouds();
                const vignette = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
                vignette.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
                vignette.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.18)');
                ctx.fillStyle = vignette;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
            }

            function stars_initScene() {
                stars_stars = stars_generateStars(W, H);
                stars_clouds = stars_generateClouds(W, H);
            }

            // ================================================================
            // 蛋糕过渡 (CAKE_TRANSITION)
            // ================================================================
            function startCakeTransition() {
                currentPhase = MASTER_PHASE.CAKE_TRANSITION;
                cakeTransitionProgress = 0;
                phaseStartTime = performance.now();
                infoEl.classList.remove('fireworks');
                infoEl.classList.remove('show');
                statusTip.textContent = '✦ 许个愿吧 · 星河祝福 ✦';
                statusTip.classList.add('show');

                // ★ 核心修改：进入蛋糕阶段前，强制停止所有音效
                if (soundEngine) {
                    soundEngine.stopAll(); // 停止所有正在播放的声音
                    // 重置所有声音 ID，避免残留引用
                    activeShowerSoundIds = [];
                    meteorSoundId = null;
                    currentCitySoundId = null;
                    currentSpaceSoundId = null;
                    heartbeatSoundId = null;
                    birthdaySoundId = null; // 重置生日歌ID，确保重新播放
                    console.log('🔇 已停止所有音效，准备播放生日歌');
                }

                // ★ 进入蛋糕场景自动播放生日歌 (循环 + 完整音频)
                playBirthdayMusic();

                initCakeFlow();
            }

            function updateCakeTransition(timestamp) {
                const elapsed = timestamp - phaseStartTime;
                cakeTransitionProgress = Math.min(elapsed / CONFIG_MASTER.cakeTransitionDuration, 1);
                if (cakeTransitionProgress >= 1) {
                    currentPhase = MASTER_PHASE.CAKE_FLOW;
                    cakeFlowStarted = true;
                    startCakeFlow();
                    statusTip.classList.remove('show');
                }
            }

            function drawCakeTransition(timestamp) {
                const p = cakeTransitionProgress;
                drawFwScene(timestamp / 100);
                if (p > 0) {
                    const alpha = easeInOutQuad(p);
                    ctx.save();
                    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
                    drawCakeToMain();
                    ctx.restore();
                    if (p < 0.3) {
                        const flash = (1 - p / 0.3) * 0.3;
                        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${flash})`;
                        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
                    }
                }
            }

            // ================================================================
            // 蛋糕流程 (CAKE_FLOW)
            // ================================================================
            let cakeCloseState = {
                progress: 0,
                phase: 'idle',
                startTime: 0,
                isClosed: false,
                isOpening: false,
            };
            const CAKE_CLOSE_DURATION = CONFIG_MASTER.cakeCloseDuration;
            const CAKE_HOLD_DURATION = CONFIG_MASTER.cakeHoldDuration;
            const CAKE_OPEN_DURATION = CONFIG_MASTER.cakeOpenDuration;

            let cakeBlowDone = false;
            let cakeSmokeStartTime = 0;
            const CAKE_SMOKE_WAIT_DURATION = CONFIG_MASTER.smokeWaitDuration;
            let cakeTransitionPhase = 'idle';
            let cakeTransitionStart = 0;
            const CAKE_TRANSITION_DURATION = CONFIG_MASTER.cakeTransitionDuration;
            let cakeTransitionAlpha = 0;
            let cakeScene = 'cake';
            let particlesReady = false;
            let particleState = 'idle';
            let allParticles = [];
            let blessings = [];
            let targetCharPositions = [];
            let flyStartTime = 0;

            const BASE_BLESSINGS = [
                '愿你眼中有星辰大海', '心中有繁花似锦', '前路漫漫亦灿灿', '做自己的光', '温柔且坚定',
                '未来皆可期', '感谢青春有你', '去更远的地方', '永远年轻，永远热泪盈眶', '生日快乐',
                '愿你所遇皆温柔', '岁月如歌', '前程似锦', '万事胜意', '未来可期', '平安喜乐',
                '得偿所愿', '光芒万丈', '不负韶华', '以梦为马', '乘风破浪', '星河长明',
                '万事顺遂', '人间值得', '山河无恙', '人间皆安'
            ];
            let BLESSINGS = [];
            while (BLESSINGS.length < PARTICLE_CFG.particlesPerChar * 4) {
                BASE_BLESSINGS.forEach(t => {
                    if (BLESSINGS.length < PARTICLE_CFG.particlesPerChar * 4) BLESSINGS.push(t);
                });
            }
            for (let i = BLESSINGS.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
                [BLESSINGS[i], BLESSINGS[j]] = [BLESSINGS[j], BLESSINGS[i]];
            }

            const particleTex = (function () {
                const c = document.createElement('canvas');
                const size = 28;
                c.width = size;
                c.height = size;
                const cx = c.getContext('2d');
                const grad = cx.createRadialGradient(size / 2, size / 2, 0, size / 2, size / 2, size / 2);
                grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)');
                grad.addColorStop(0.3, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.95)');
                grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
                cx.fillStyle = grad;
                cx.arc(size / 2, size / 2, size / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                cx.fill();
                return c;
            })();

            const offscreen = document.createElement('canvas');
            const offCtx2 = offscreen.getContext('2d');

            function extractCharPixels(char, fontSize, targetCount) {
                const pad = 16,
                    size = fontSize + pad * 2;
                offscreen.width = size;
                offscreen.height = size;
                offCtx2.clearRect(0, 0, size, size);
                offCtx2.textAlign = 'center';
                offCtx2.textBaseline = 'middle';
                offCtx2.font = `bold ${fontSize}px "PingFang SC","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif`;
                offCtx2.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
                offCtx2.fillText(char, size / 2, size / 2);
                const imgData = offCtx2.getImageData(0, 0, size, size);
                const data = imgData.data;
                const raw = [];
                for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
                    for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
                        const idx = (y * size + x) * 4;
                        if (data[idx + 3] > 30) raw.push({
                            x: x - pad,
                            y: y - pad,
                            r: data[idx],
                            g: data[idx + 1],
                            b: data[idx + 2]
                        });
                    }
                }
                if (raw.length > 0) {
                    let minX = Infinity,
                        maxX = -Infinity,
                        minY = Infinity,
                        maxY = -Infinity;
                    raw.forEach(p => {
                        if (p.x < minX) minX = p.x;
                        if (p.x > maxX) maxX = p.x;
                        if (p.y < minY) minY = p.y;
                        if (p.y > maxY) maxY = p.y;
                    });
                    const cx = (minX + maxX) / 2,
                        cy = (minY + maxY) / 2;
                    raw.forEach(p => {
                        p.x -= cx;
                        p.y -= cy;
                    });
                }
                if (raw.length === 0) {
                    for (let i = 0; i < targetCount; i++) raw.push({
                        x: rand(-fontSize / 2, fontSize / 2),
                        y: rand(-fontSize / 2, fontSize / 2),
                        r: 255,
                        g: 215,
                        b: 50
                    });
                }
                const result = [];
                const shuffled = [...raw];
                for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                    const j = randInt(0, i);
                    [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
                }
                for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(targetCount, shuffled.length); i++) result.push(shuffled[i]);
                while (result.length < targetCount) {
                    const p = raw[randInt(0, raw.length - 1)];
                    result.push({ x: p.x + rand(-3, 3), y: p.y + rand(-3, 3), r: p.r, g: p.g, b: p.b });
                }
                return result;
            }

            function buildTargetPositions() {
                const chars = ['生', '日', '快', '乐'];
                const fs = PARTICLE_CFG.targetFontSize,
                    gap = PARTICLE_CFG.charGap;
                const charWidth = fs * 0.62;
                const totalW = chars.length * charWidth + (chars.length - 1) * gap;
                const startX = (W - totalW) / 2;
                const centerY = H * 0.30;
                const allPos = [];
                chars.forEach((ch, ci) => {
                    const px = extractCharPixels(ch, fs, PARTICLE_CFG.particlesPerChar);
                    const cx = startX + ci * (charWidth + gap) + charWidth / 2;
                    px.forEach(p => {
                        allPos.push({
                            x: cx + p.x,
                            y: centerY + p.y,
                            r: p.r || 255,
                            g: p.g || 215,
                            b: p.b || 50,
                            group: ci
                        });
                    });
                });
                for (let g = 0; g < 4; g++) {
                    const groupItems = allPos.filter(p => p.group === g);
                    for (let i = groupItems.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                        const j = randInt(0, i);
                        [groupItems[i], groupItems[j]] = [groupItems[j], groupItems[i]];
                    }
                }
                return allPos;
            }

            function createBlessingItem(text, index, total, targetPos, startDelay) {
                const fs = rand(PARTICLE_CFG.fontSizeMin, PARTICLE_CFG.fontSizeMax);
                const dirs = ['left', 'right', 'top', 'bottom'];
                const dir = dirs[index % dirs.length];
                const margin = 50;
                let sx, sy;
                switch (dir) {
                    case 'left':
                        sx = -margin - fs * text.length * 0.35;
                        sy = rand(H * 0.08, H * 0.92);
                        break;
                    case 'right':
                        sx = W + margin + fs * text.length * 0.35;
                        sy = rand(H * 0.08, H * 0.92);
                        break;
                    case 'top':
                        sx = rand(W * 0.05, W * 0.95);
                        sy = -margin - fs;
                        break;
                    case 'bottom':
                        sx = rand(W * 0.05, W * 0.95);
                        sy = H + margin + fs;
                        break;
                }
                return {
                    text,
                    fontSize: fs,
                    startX: sx,
                    startY: sy,
                    targetX: targetPos.x,
                    targetY: targetPos.y,
                    cpX: (sx + targetPos.x) / 2 + rand(-W * 0.15, W * 0.15),
                    cpY: (sy + targetPos.y) / 2 + rand(-H * 0.15, H * 0.15),
                    index,
                    progress: 0,
                    done: false,
                    particleSize: rand(PARTICLE_CFG.particleSizeMin, PARTICLE_CFG.particleSizeMax),
                    color: { r: 255, g: 240, b: 200 },
                    group: targetPos.group,
                    startDelay,
                    activated: false
                };
            }

            function initParticles() {
                const targetPos = buildTargetPositions();
                const count = Math.min(BLESSINGS.length, targetPos.length);
                const groupBaseDelay = [0, 1500, 3000, 4500];
                targetCharPositions = [];
                const fs = PARTICLE_CFG.targetFontSize,
                    gap = PARTICLE_CFG.charGap;
                const charWidth = fs * 0.62;
                const totalW = 4 * charWidth + 3 * gap;
                const startX = (W - totalW) / 2;
                const centerY = H * 0.30;
                ['生', '日', '快', '乐'].forEach((ch, i) => {
                    const cx = startX + i * (charWidth + gap) + charWidth / 2;
                    targetCharPositions.push({ char: ch, x: cx, y: centerY, fontSize: fs });
                });
                blessings = [];
                const groupTotal = [0, 0, 0, 0];
                const groupProgress = [0, 0, 0, 0];
                targetPos.forEach(pos => { if (pos.group >= 0 && pos.group < 4) groupTotal[pos.group]++; });
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    const pos = targetPos[i % targetPos.length];
                    const base = groupBaseDelay[pos.group] || 0;
                    const idxInGroup = groupProgress[pos.group]++;
                    const currentGroupTotal = groupTotal[pos.group];
                    const t = idxInGroup / currentGroupTotal;
                    const timeInGroup = t * PARTICLE_CFG.jitterMax;
                    const startDelay = base + Math.max(0, timeInGroup);
                    blessings.push(createBlessingItem(BLESSINGS[i], i, count, pos, startDelay));
                }
                allParticles = blessings.map(b => ({
                    x: 0,
                    y: 0,
                    targetX: b.targetX,
                    targetY: b.targetY,
                    size: b.particleSize,
                    r: b.color.r,
                    g: b.color.g,
                    b: b.color.b,
                    alpha: 0,
                    progress: 0,
                    startDelay: b.startDelay,
                    group: b.group,
                    activated: false,
                    done: false
                }));
                particleState = 'idle';
                flyStartTime = 0;
                blessings.forEach(b => {
                    b.progress = 0;
                    b.done = false;
                    b.activated = false;
                });
                allParticles.forEach(p => {
                    p.alpha = 0;
                    p.progress = 0;
                    p.activated = false;
                    p.done = false;
                });
                particlesReady = true;
            }

            function drawParticle(p) {
                if (p.alpha < 0.01) return;
                const size = p.size;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.alpha * PARTICLE_CFG.stableParticleAlpha, 0, 1);
                ctx.drawImage(particleTex, p.x - size / 2, p.y - size / 2, size, size);
                ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
                ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,230,180,0.8)`;
                ctx.fillRect(p.x - size / 2, p.y - size / 2, size, size);
                ctx.restore();
            }

            function drawGlowText(text, x, y, fs, alpha, blur, color) {
                alpha = alpha || 1;
                blur = blur || 40;
                color = color || 'rgba(255,215,0,0.5)';
                if (alpha < 0.05 || fs < 3) return;
                ctx.save();
                ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                ctx.font = `bold ${fs}px "PingFang SC","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif`;
                ctx.shadowColor = color;
                ctx.shadowBlur = blur;
                ctx.fillStyle = PARTICLE_CFG.textColor;
                ctx.fillText(text, x, y);
                ctx.restore();
            }

            // === 移动端适配 — drawStableGlow 使用 PARTICLE_CFG 中的动态值 ===
            function drawStableGlow() {
                const fs = PARTICLE_CFG.targetFontSize;
                const gap = PARTICLE_CFG.charGap;
                const chars = ['生', '日', '快', '乐'];
                const charWidth = fs * 0.62;
                const totalW = chars.length * charWidth + (chars.length - 1) * gap;
                const startX = (W - totalW) / 2;
                const centerY = H * 0.30;

                ctx.save();
                for (let i = 0; i < chars.length; i++) {
                    const x = startX + i * (charWidth + gap) + charWidth / 2;
                    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                    ctx.font = `bold ${fs}px "PingFang SC","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif`;
                    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x - fs / 2, centerY, x + fs / 2, centerY);
                    grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffd700');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, '#ffb6c1');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, '#87ceeb');
                    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,200,100,0.7)';
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 50;
                    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                    ctx.fillText(chars[i], x, centerY);
                }
                ctx.restore();
            }

            function updateParticles(timestamp) {
                if (particleState !== 'flying') return;
                if (flyStartTime === 0) flyStartTime = timestamp;
                const elapsed = timestamp - flyStartTime;
                let allDone = true;
                allParticles.forEach((p, idx) => {
                    const b = blessings[idx];
                    if (!p.activated && elapsed >= p.startDelay) {
                        p.activated = true;
                        b.activated = true;
                    }
                    if (!p.activated) { allDone = false; return; }
                    if (p.done) return;
                    const localElapsed = elapsed - p.startDelay;
